window.FARGHANI_CRITICAL_PASS_001={
  "schema": "gnamma-farghani-critical-pass-clause-v1",

  "status": "precritical-source-verification-only",
  "records": [
    {
      "id": "CR001-001",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "الأصل الأول في ذكر رتب الذات وتعيّن الأسماء منها والصفات وذكر كيفية انتشاء الفروع من تلك الأسماء والصفات من أصولها مع تعرّض لشرح الأسماء التسعة والتسعين ، وذكر انتشاء باقي الأسماء منها دقيقها وجليلها",
      "en": "First foundation: the ranks of the Essence, the determination of names and attributes from it, the derivation of their branches from their roots, an account of the ninety-nine names, and the derivation of the other names, subtle and great.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The older English said 'differentiation' for تعيّن; preserve the technical 'determination'.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-002",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "بِسْمِ اللَّهِ الرَّحْمنِ الرَّحِيمِ نقل عن رسول اللّه صلّى اللّه عليه وسلّم أنه قال : « قال اللّه عزّ وجلّ : كنت كنزا مخفيّا فأحببت أن أعرف ، فخلقت الخلق لأعرف »",
      "en": "In the name of God, the All-merciful, the Especially Merciful. It is transmitted as from the Messenger of God that God said: 'I was a hidden treasure, and I loved to be known, so I created creation in order to be known.'",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī reports an attribution; the 'hidden treasure' saying lacks an established prophetic isnād. Its interpretive importance must not be treated as authentication.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-003",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "اعلم أيّدك اللّه بالفهم الصحيح أنه قد كنى في هذا الحديث الإلهي عن كنه الغيب وإطلاق الذات الأقدس وباطن الهوية الأزلية بالكنز المخفي",
      "en": "Know—may God strengthen you with sound understanding—that the hidden treasure in this divine saying figuratively signifies the innermost reality of the Unseen, the nondelimitation of the most holy Essence, and the inward aspect of eternal Identity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep kunh (inner reality) distinct from its iṭlāq (nondelimitation).",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-004",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "وذلك لملابسة أن الكنز عبارة عن عين مغيب مكنون وسرّ مخزون مشتمل على جواهر عظيمة الجدوى مرغوب فيها",
      "en": "The analogy arises because a treasure is a concealed and guarded thing, a secret held in reserve, comprising precious jewels whose benefits are great and which are much sought after.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The prior paraphrase omitted the explicit 'great benefit' (ʿaẓīmat al-jadwā) and being sought (marghūb fīhā).",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-005",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "ما بين أنفس تصان ويضنّ ويستأثر بها وبين نفيس ، ربما يقع فيه التسامح والتبدّل خصوصا أو عموما",
      "en": "Some of these are so precious that they are guarded, withheld and reserved to their possessor; others are precious but may be dealt with more freely or exchanged, privately or generally.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The second comparison is between anfas and nafīs, degrees of relative preciousness.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-006",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "ومهما أريد إظهاره يعرض أولا عرضا مجملا بحيث يبين الكنز ويظهر لواجده في محله",
      "en": "When it is desired to disclose a treasure, it is first displayed as a whole, so that the treasure becomes evident to its finder in its own place.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Preserve ijmāl (undifferentiated comprehensiveness), not simply a generic presentation.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-007",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "ثم يعرض ثانيا عرضا يميّز فيه بين الأنفس والنفيس ، وينقل منه عن محله لحكمة يراها الواجد من إظهار كماله الذي يتضمّن غناءه وإظهار التجمّل والجود ونحو ذلك",
      "en": "It is then shown a second time in a display that differentiates what is most precious from what is merely precious; some is removed from its place according to a wisdom perceived by the finder, to display his perfection, including wealth, adornment, generosity and similar qualities.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "A figurative sequence of comprehensive and then differentiated disclosure; not a temporal sequence in the divine Essence.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-008",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "وكذلك كنه الجناب الأقدس تعالى وتقدّس وله المثل الأعلى في السماوات والأرض وهو العزيز الحكيم ، بلا نسبة تشبيه وتشكيل وتمثيل إليه تعالى عن ذلك علوّا كبيرا",
      "en": "Likewise with the innermost reality of the most holy Presence—exalted and sanctified is He, to whom belongs the highest similitude in the heavens and earth, the Mighty and Wise—without attributing to Him any likeness, configuration or representation. He is vastly exalted above that.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The anti-anthropomorphic qualification is part of the authorial analogy, not an editorial gloss.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-009",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "فإنه الغيب المكنون والسرّ المصون الذي هو أبطن كل باطن",
      "en": "For He is the hidden Unseen and the protected secret, more inward than every inward thing.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This is a divine negative-theological predication in analogy.",

      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

      "en": "His inwardness contains the jewels of the essential names, the most precious of all names.",

    {
      "id": "CR001-011",
      "page": 18,
      "ar": "التي منها ما يستأثر في مكنون الغيب عنده ، فلا يعلمها إلّا هو ومن ارتفعت بينونته ورفعت كينونته ممن هو أكمل جميع",
      "en": "Among them are names He reserves in the hidden mystery with Him; none knows them except He and one whose separateness and individual selfhood have been lifted away, one of the most perfect among all—",

      "page": 19,

      "id": "CR001-013",
      "page": 19,

      "en": "It also contains the precious jewels of the names of attributes, acquaintance with which is permitted to anyone suited to be honored by them, whether one who has adopted their character or a perfected person who has realized them.",

    {

      "id": "CR001-016",
      "page": 19,
      "ar": "فإن مفهوم تاء المتكلّم عن نفسه يؤذن بتعيّنه في نفسه وظهوره حالة تكلّمه تعيّنا وظهورا",
      "en": "The first-person tāʾ, by which the speaker speaks of himself, implies his determination in himself and his manifestation when speaking, as determination and manifestation.",

      "source_siglum": "B2007",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-017",
      "page": 19,
      "ar": "أما قوله : « كنزا مخفيّا » ينبئ عن غيبه وإطلاقه بحيث لا يدخل تحت حكم معيّن يحكم عليه من حيث هذا الغيب والإطلاق",
      "en": "By contrast, 'a hidden treasure' signifies His unseen character and nondelimitation, in which no determinate judgment can be applied to Him precisely insofar as He is unseen and undelimited.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The qualification min ḥayth is crucial; not an unrestricted negation of divine predication.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-018",
      "page": 19,
      "ar": "ولا تحت إحاطة به علما من حيث تعيّناته أصلا ، فإنّ مطلق الخفاء يحكم بذلك كلّه",
      "en": "Nor can He be encompassed by knowledge through His determinations in this respect; absolute hiddenness entails all this.",
      "disposition": "retain",

      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,

    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-019",
      "page": 19,
      "ar": "وحيث كان الخفاء وقع خبرا « لكنت » عرف سبق الإطلاق والغيب على ذلك التعيّن ، ولكن سبقا رتبيّا لا زمانيّا ، فإنه صحّ أنه ليس عند اللّه صباح ولا مساء",
      "en": "Since hiddenness is predicated of 'I was', nondelimitation and the Unseen are understood to precede that determination—but by priority of rank, not priority in time; for there is neither morning nor evening with God.",

      "source_siglum": "B2007",

      "en": "'Then I loved' tells of an original inclination linking the hiddenness and manifestation just described.",

      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

    },
    {

      "ar": "وما ثمّ إلّا التعيّن الذي هو عين الوحدة ، فكان عينه",

      "ar": "ليتحقّق كمال ذلك الظهور مفصّلا متميّزا ويشمل حكمه وأثره المقدّر والمقدّر تعقّبا ذاتيّا لا زمانيّا ، بل عمليّا ووجوديّا أيضا بالنسبة إلى المقدّر العالم الأزلي وعلمه الذاتي والصفاتي الأبدي",
      "en": "So that the perfection of disclosure may be realized in articulated distinction, and its rule and effect may encompass the determiner and what is determined in essential rather than temporal succession—and also in knowledge and existence with respect to the eternal Determiner who knows, and His everlasting essential and attributive knowledge.",
      "disposition": "corrected",
      "critical_note": "**Historical-print correction:** I1876 printed p6, PDF scan 10, reads بل علميا ووجوديا; B2007 digital prints بل عمليّا ووجوديّا.       "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false,
      "historical_print": {
        "siglum": "I1876",
        "printed_page": 6,
        "scan_index": 10,
        "reading": "بل علميا ووجوديا أيضا",
        "status": "historical-print-difference",
        "source_pdf": "https://mc.dlib.nyu.edu/files/books/princeton_aco000264/princeton_aco000264_lo.pdf",
        "evidence_register": "editorial/I1876_COLLATION_001.json",
        "note": "I1876 p6 visibly reads علميا where B2007 digital reads عمليا. Retain historical print reading and existing epistemic/existential sense.",
        "verified_direct_photo": true,
        "manuscript_verified": false
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-025",
      "page": 19,
      "ar": "فإن علمه عين وجوده ، فافهم تقرّ بالكبريت الأحمر والكنز الأكبر إن شاء اللّه تعالى",
      "en": "For His knowledge is identical with His existence. Understand, and—God willing—you will rejoice in the red sulphur and the greatest treasure.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The independent clause returns to the main metaphysical assertion.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-026",
      "page": 19,
      "ar": "ثم اعلم أن كنه الذات الأقدس وغيب الهوية والإطلاق والأزلية المندرج فيه حكم الأبدية لا يشهد ولا يفهم ولا يعلم ولا يدرك من حيث التعيّن أصلا",
      "en": "Know further that the innermost reality of the most holy Essence, the hiddenness of Identity, nondelimitation and pre-eternity—in which the determination of everlastingness is included—cannot be witnessed, understood, known or apprehended from the standpoint of any determination whatsoever.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Important: the denial concerns apprehension of the undelimited Essence as such, not all knowledge of God through self-disclosure.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-027",
      "page": 19,
      "ar": "ولا يدخل تحت حكم متعيّن البتّة . نعم اللّهمّ إلا حكما سلبيّا بأنها لا تعلم",
      "en": "Nor can it fall under any determinate judgment at all—except, perhaps, a negative judgment that it is not knowable.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The limiting exception is not an affirmative grasp of the Essence.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-028",
      "page": 19,
      "ar": "وإنما توجه وتأتى هذا الحكم السلبي أيضا بأحد وجهين ؛ أحدهما : إخبار إلهي عن ذلك",
      "en": "Even this negative judgment can be directed or attained only in one of two ways. The first is divine report concerning it—",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The sentence continues on p20.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-029",
      "page": 20,
      "ar": "بقوله تعالى : وَلا يُحِيطُونَ بِهِ عِلْماً [ طه : الآية 110 ] ، وبقوله : « كنت كنزا مخفيّا » ونحو ذلك",
      "en": "—as in God's words, 'They do not encompass Him in knowledge' (Qurʾān 20:110), and the saying 'I was a hidden treasure', and similar expressions.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish an actual Qurʾānic verse from the unattested 'hidden treasure' saying, though Farghānī lists both as divine report.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-030",
      "page": 20,
      "ar": "والوجه الثاني : انقطاع أكمل الإدراكات والعلوم وأوسع الشهودات والفهوم إليه واعترافه بالعجز والقصور والحيرة فيه",
      "en": "The second way is for the most perfect apprehensions and sciences, and the widest witnessings and understandings, to come to their limit before Him and confess their incapacity, limitation and bewilderment concerning Him.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not construe 'confession of incapacity' as ignorance of every divine name.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-031",
      "page": 20,
      "ar": "وبأن ما وراء غاية مدركه ونهاية مشهده بحار يحار في تيّارها فهم كل غائص واقف ، ويتيه في تيه كنهها وغور أسرارها علم كل خائض عارف",
      "en": "They acknowledge that beyond the furthest object of apprehension and the last horizon of witnessing are seas whose currents bewilder every diver's understanding, and deserts whose hidden depths confound the knowledge of every knowing traveler.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Preserves the two images, seas and trackless wilderness.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-032",
      "page": 20,
      "ar": "هي المقصد الأعلى والمطلب الأولى ؛ كما نصّ صلّى اللّه تعالى عليه وسلم بقوله في مناجاته : « لا أحصى ثناء عليك »",
      "en": "These are the highest end and most worthy object of seeking; the Prophet indicated this in his supplication, 'I cannot enumerate the praise due to You.'",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The poetic plural 'these' refers back to inaccessible depths, not a positive list of predicates.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-033",
      "page": 20,
      "ar": "لا أبلغ كل ما فيك ، وبقوله أيضا في أثناء دعائه : « أو استأثرت به في مكنون الغيب عندك »",
      "en": "That is, 'I cannot reach all that is in You'; and likewise in his supplication, 'or [a name] that You have reserved in the hidden mystery with You'.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "'I cannot enumerate Your praise' appears in Ṣaḥīḥ Muslim 486; distinguish Farghānī's gloss from the hadith's words. The reserved names phrase comes from another supplication.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-034",
      "page": 20,
      "ar": "وبقوله : « ربّ زدني فيك تحيّرا » ، وأمثال ذلك",
      "en": "And in the saying 'My Lord, increase me in bewilderment concerning You', and similar reports.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Modern editor of B2007 p20 explicitly says he did not find this report in his sources; do not imply an established hadith.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-035",
      "page": 20,
      "ar": "ومهما علم أو شوهد أو أدرك شيء منها عند تجلّيها الظاهري أو الباطني أو الجمعي",
      "en": "Whatever of these realities is known, witnessed or apprehended through their outward, inward or comprehensive self-disclosure—",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The clause is conditional and the construction continues over the printed page boundary.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR001-036",
      "page": 20,
      "ar": "في السير الحبي وقرب النوافل ، وتقدم السلوك على الجذبة وسبق الفناء على البقاء",
      "en": "—in the journey of love and nearness through supererogatory works, where disciplined journeying precedes attraction and annihilation precedes subsistence—",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep distinctions: ḥubbī versus maḥbūbī and nawāfil versus farāʾiḍ, developed on p21.",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar",

      "en": "—then at the spiritual opening it becomes apparent that the self-disclosing Real is the instrument by which the servant apprehends—",

      "disposition": "retain",

    {
      "id": "CR002-002",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "وفي السير المحبوبي وقرب الفرائض وتأخّر السلوك عن الجذبة وتقدم البقاء الأصلي على الفناء",
      "en": "In the journey of the beloved and the nearness of obligatory acts, where traveling follows attraction and original subsistence precedes annihilation—",

      "critical_note": "A reversal of the order assigned to the ḥubbī journey at p20.",

      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

    {
      "id": "CR002-003",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "حيث يتبيّن أن العبد المتجلّى له آلة لإدراك الحق المتجلّي من باب « فإن اللّه تعالى قال على لسان عبده : سمع اللّه تعالى لمن حمده »",
      "en": "—it becomes clear that the servant who receives disclosure is the instrument of the self-disclosing Real's apprehension, in the sense of 'God speaks on His servant's tongue: God hears the one who praises Him.'",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Instrument-to-apprehender relation is reversed relative to the earlier sentence.",

      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-004",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "وعند انتهاء السيرين والجمع بين الحكمين ابتداء وانتهاء حيث يصح أن كل واحد منهما مدركا وآلة على التعاقب أو معا في حالة واحدة من باب وَما رَمَيْتَ إِذْ رَمَيْتَ وَلكِنَّ اللَّهَ رَمى",
      "en": "At the completion of both journeys and their union in beginning and end, each can be the apprehender and the instrument, successively or together in one state, as in 'You did not throw when you threw, but God threw' (Qurʾān 8:17).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The subject is the reciprocal grammatical distribution of apprehender and instrument, not equality of independent beings.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-005",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "فعلى كل حال يكون ذلك العلم والشهود والتجلّي والإدراك من حيث تعينه ومشيئته وعلمه المقدّس بذاته الأقدس تعالى وتقدّس من حيث واحديتها ، لا من حيث إطلاقها وأحديتها",
      "en": "In every case, this knowing, witnessing, disclosure and apprehension concern His determination, His will and His sacred knowledge of His most holy Essence—exalted and sanctified is He—under the consideration of its oneness (wāḥidiyya), not under that of its nondelimitation and unicity (aḥadiyya).",

      "source_siglum": "B2007",

      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

    {
      "id": "CR002-006",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "فإن إدراكه وشهوده من حيث تعيّنه وعلمه غير ممنوع ، بل موكول إلى مشيئته تعالى وتقدّس كما قال عزّ من قائل : وَلا يُحِيطُونَ بِشَيْءٍ مِنْ عِلْمِهِ إِلَّا بِما شاءَ",
      "en": "For apprehending and witnessing Him under the consideration of His determination and knowledge is not prohibited; it is dependent upon His will, as He says, 'They encompass none of His knowledge except what He wills' (Qurʾān 2:255).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The scope qualifier is essential to avoid treating the Essence's undelimitation as an object of determined knowledge.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-007",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "ثم إن وحدته التي انتشئت منها الأحديّة والواحدية هي عين ذاته لا صفة زائدة ولا نعت زائد عليها",
      "en": "His unity, from which unicity and oneness derive, is identical with His Essence: it is neither an additional attribute nor an additional qualification.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not identify two considerations with two distinct divine substances.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-008",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "لكن للوحدة اعتباران أصليّان أوليّان ، أحدهما : سقوط الاعتبارات عنها بالكلية ، وسمّي الذات أحدا بهذا الاعتبار ومتعلقة بطون الذات وإطلاقها وأزليّتها",
      "en": "But unity admits two original, primary considerations. The first is the complete dropping away of considerations, under which the Essence is called One in unicity (aḥad), related to its hiddenness, nondelimitation and pre-eternity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Important to separate first determination (comprehensive intermediary) from aḥadiyya (one consideration).",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-009",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "وعلى هذا يكون نسبة الاسم الأحد إلى السلب أحقّ من نسبته إلى الثبوت والإيجاب",
      "en": "Thus the name aḥad is more properly related to negation than to establishment and affirmation.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Metaphysical status of the negative consideration.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-010",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "والاعتبار الثاني : ثبوت الاعتبارات الغير المتناهية لها مع اندراجها فيها في أول رتبة الذات ، وتحقّق أكثر تفصيل تعيّناتها في ثاني الرتبة الآتي بيان كليهما عن قريب إن شاء اللّه تعالى",
      "en": "The second consideration is the establishment of infinitely many considerations for unity, included within it at the first rank of the Essence, while most of their determinate differentiation is realized at the second rank, as both will shortly be explained.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not read second-rank explicit differentiation back into the first-rank inclusion.",

      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-011",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "كالنصفية والثلثية والربعية الثابتة والمندرجة في هذا الواحد المشهود ههنا ، التي ينتشىء منها الأعداد في المراتب الوجودية",
      "en": "Like halfness, thirdness and quarterness, established and contained in the one considered here, from which numbers arise in existential ranks.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Numerical analogy illustrates included relations, not arithmetic divisions within the Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-012",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "وينتشىء من تلك الاعتبارات أعيان الاثنين والثلاثة والأربعة فيها ، كما يقال : الواحد نصف الاثنين ، وثلث الثلاثة ، وربع الأربعة وهلمّ جرّا",
      "en": "And from these considerations arise the distinct two, three and four: the one is half of two, a third of three, a quarter of four, and so on.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep ʿayn as determinate identity here, not the same sense as externally existing individual.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-013",
      "page": 21,
      "ar": "والذات بهذا الاعتبار يسمّى واحدا اسما ثبوتيّا لا سلبيّا ، ولم تصح إضافة هذه الاعتبارات المذكورة إلى الاسم الأحد لا حقيقة ولا وضعا لغويّا",
      "en": "Under this consideration the Essence is called wāḥid, an affirmative rather than a negative name. These considerations are not properly attributed to the name aḥad, either in reality or by linguistic convention.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The last phrase continues in the next printed page with 'فحقائق هذه الاعتبارات'.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-014",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "الاعتبارات في أول رتبة الذات ثابتة للاسم الواحد الحقيقي ، ومندرجة فيه ثبوتا حقيقيّا واندراجا أصليّا",
      "en": "These considerations at the first rank of the Essence are established for the truly One and included within it by real establishment and original inclusion.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Source presents true thubūt but does not equate it with external existential actuality.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-015",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "كما أن أمثلتها وصورها ثابتة لهذا الواحد المشهود أثره لنا ههنا ، ومندرجة فيه غير قادحة في الرتبتين في صرافة هذا الواحد ، ولا منافية لها",
      "en": "As their analogues and forms are established within the one whose effect is witnessed here, their inclusion at either rank neither impairs nor contradicts the simplicity of that one.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The clause explicitly controls the number analogy's ontological limits.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-016",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "ومتعلق هذا الاعتبار الواحدي ظهور الذات ووجودها وأبديّتها ؛ كما أن متعلق الاعتبار الأحدي بطونها وأزليّتها",
      "en": "The consideration of oneness concerns the Essence's manifestation, existence and perpetuity; the consideration of unicity concerns its hiddenness and pre-eternity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Preserve the parallel between ẓuhūr / buṭūn and abad / azal.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-017",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "ولا مغايرة ولا غيرية بين هذين الاعتبارين ، ولا بين كل اعتبار واعتبار من اعتبارات الواحدية في أول رتبة الذات الآتي بيانه",
      "en": "There is neither differentiation nor otherness between these two considerations, nor between one consideration of oneness and another at the first rank of the Essence.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish mughāyara and ghayriyya as both negated, not just simple sameness.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-018",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "لأن المغايرة والغيرية من أحكام الكثرة والمنافاة الثابتة بينها وبين الوحدة ، ولا كثرة ثمة تنافي الوحدة ، بل تلك الوحدة هي المنشئة لكل وحدة وكثرة مفهومة ههنا متغايرة عندنا",
      "en": "For difference and otherness are judgments of multiplicity and its opposition to unity. There, no multiplicity exists that opposes unity; that unity is the source of every unity and multiplicity understood here as different.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This does not by itself negate real plurality in subsequent manifested ranks.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-019",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "ولا يفهم ارتفاع هذه المغايرة في تلك الحضرة إلّا من ارتفع حالا ومقاما عن التقيّد بالمراتب المقيّدة بأحكام الكثرة والمقيّدة لأربابها بها",
      "en": "None understands the removal of such difference in that Presence unless he has risen, in state and station, beyond restriction by ranks determined by judgments of multiplicity and restricting their occupants through them.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Explicit link between ontological rank and transformed apprehension.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-020",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "ولارتفاع حكم هذه المغايرة في الرتبة الأولى حكم بها بعض أكابر المحقّقين من أهل اللّه بأن الواحد الأحد اسم واحد مركّب ؛ كبعلبك ومعديكرب",
      "en": "Because the judgment of difference is absent at the first rank, some eminent realized masters hold that al-wāḥid al-aḥad is one compound name, like Baʿlabakk or Maʿdīkarib.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Grammatical examples are genuine proper-name compounds; not a third ontological rank.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-021",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "ثم اعلم أن هذه الاعتبارات الثابتة للواحد ثمة ، والمندرجة فيه في أول رتبة الذات المحكوم عليها فيها بأنها عين الذات",
      "en": "Know, further, that these considerations established for the One and included within it at the first rank of the Essence are judged there to be the Essence itself.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Differentiation at later ranks must not be inserted into this first-rank claim.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-022",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "بحيث إن كل ما يضاف إلى الذات يضاف إلى كلّ واحد منها من الجمعية والاشتمال على الكلّ في هذه الرتبة الأولى بحسبها",
      "en": "Thus what is attributed to the Essence under the aspect of comprehensiveness and containing the whole is likewise attributed to each of the considerations at this rank.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The qualifier 'at this rank' is essential.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-023",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "نظرا من حيث مظاهرها وصورها وآثارها إليها ألفينا بعضها كلّيات وأصولا أول بمثابة أجناس عالية",
      "en": "If considered through their manifestations, forms and effects, some prove to be universals and primary principles comparable to supreme genera.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Move from first-rank inclusion to what follows upon later manifestation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-024",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "هي مسمّى ألفاظ دالّة على حقائق أسماء الذات ومفهوماتها التي منها مفاتح الغيب ، والواحد الأحد وباطن اسم اللّه ، وباطن اسم الرحمن الرحيم ، ومفهوم جمع أسماء الضمائر",
      "en": "These are the realities named by words designating the essential names and their concepts: the Keys of the Unseen, the One-in-Unicity, the inward aspect of Allāh, the inward aspect of al-Raḥmān and al-Raḥīm, and the comprehensive meaning of pronominal names.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish name-realities from their linguistic words, elaborated at later source pages.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-025",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "ووجدنا بعض تلك الاعتبارات المندرجة في الواحدية من حيث النظر في مظاهرها وآثارها بمثابة أجناس تالية وأنواع مندرجة فيها وهلمّ جرّا إلى أشخاص هاوية إلى الدّركات الجزئية جدّا",
      "en": "Some of the considerations included within oneness, considered through their effects, are like lower genera, species included within them, and so on down to individuals at the most narrowly particular levels.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Avoid treating genera and species here as independent eternally existing substances.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-026",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "التي بها يحكم على الحضرة الأبدية بعدم التناهي كثرة وتعيّنات وتقيّدات ، والكل ثابتة بصورها المعنوية في الرتبة الثانية بعضها بطريق التشخّص ، وبعضها بطريق الاندراج في الأصناف والأنواع",
      "en": "These yield the judgment that the perpetual Presence contains endlessly many determinations and restrictions. All are established as intelligible forms at the second rank, some individually, others by inclusion within kinds and species.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Explicit second-rank establishment versus first-rank inclusion.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-027",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "نعم ، وقد تعيّنت وانضبطت جملة منها في اللّوح المحفوظ ، وتصوّرت صورا وجودية روحانية فيه منتهيا ظهورها بانتهاء يوم القيامة مجملا ومفصّلا",
      "en": "A group of these is also determined and fixed in the Preserved Tablet, acquiring spiritual existential forms whose display extends to the Resurrection, both comprehensively and in detail.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Not a blanket claim that all eternal knowables manifest in this way.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-028",
      "page": 22,
      "ar": "ويتفصّل منه في المراتب الوجودية مجملا في العرش ومفصّلا في الكرسي دفعة واحدة ، ثم يتفصّل منهما في الأركان والسماوات على التعاقب إلى أنهى مراتب",
      "en": "From the Tablet it is unfolded within existential ranks, comprehensively in the Throne and in detail in the Footstool in one act, and thereafter successively in the elements and heavens down to the lowest ranks—",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The sentence runs across the p22–23 boundary; do not count any stage of manifestation as independent divinity.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-029",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "الكون مثالا وحسّا ، وفي المواطن دنيا وبرزخا وآخرة ، وينتهي إلى ههنا جميع الشهودات والإدراكات والمكاشفات الحاصلة للكمّل والأولياء",
      "en": "—of the cosmos, in imaginal and sensory form and in its worldly, intermediate and hereafter abodes. All witnessing, apprehension and unveiling attained by perfected people and saints end at this limit.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Continues p22 sentence; note distinct metaphysical modes and abodes.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-030",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "فالأدنى منهم وما فيهم أدنى ، والأعلى في حكم هذا الانتهاء سواء",
      "en": "The lowest among them and the highest are alike with respect to this limit.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The sentence denies no distinction in actual spiritual rank; only the epistemic horizon.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-031",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "إلّا أنه ربما يكون في الحضرة الغيبية الأزليّة نحو هذه الكلّيات والجزئيّات أمور باطنة وأحوال مستورة لم تتعيّن بعد لا في الرتبة الثانية ولا في اللّوح المحفوظ",
      "en": "Yet in the eternal unseen Presence there may perhaps be, regarding these universals and particulars, hidden things and concealed states not yet determined either at the second rank or in the Preserved Tablet.",
      "disposition": "corrected",
      "critical_note": "PHOTOGRAPHIC PRINT-TO-PRINT DIFFERENCE I1876 printed p8/scan12: سوى أنه ربما يكون; B2007 printed p23: إلّا أنه ربما يكون. Prefer I1876 connective provisionally; the modal ربما and the uncertain status of undetermined affairs must remain. Medieval witnesses not yet collated.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": true,
      "manuscript_verified": false,
      "historical_print": {
        "siglum": "I1876",
        "printed_page": 8,
        "scan_index": 12,
        "reading": "سوى أنه ربما يكون",
        "status": "historical-print-difference",
        "source_pdf": "https://mc.dlib.nyu.edu/files/books/princeton_aco000264/princeton_aco000264_lo.pdf",
        "hires": true,
        "photo_url": "https://github.com/gnamma-research/booksite/blob/master/texts/al-farghani/muntaha-al-madarik/facsimile-I1876-hires/images/scan_0012.jpg",
        "note": "Original-resolution historical I1876 p8 has سوى أنه, while B2007 p23 reads إلا أنه. Both are exceptive; adopt earlier print provisionally, with the Beirut reading preserved, without changing the modal nuance of ربما. No manuscript compared."
      },
      "edited_ar": "سوى أنه ربما يكون في الحضرة الغيبية الأزليّة نحو هذه الكلّيات والجزئيّات أمور باطنة وأحوال مستورة لم تتعيّن بعد لا في الرتبة الثانية ولا في اللّوح المحفوظ"
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-032",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "فلم تعلم ولم يحكم بشيء منها إلّا بعد تعيّنها ووقوعها في المراتب الوجودية بالتدريج مع الآنات",
      "en": "Accordingly they are not known or judged until their determination and occurrence in existential ranks, progressively with successive instants.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "Historical print agreement now established at I1876 pp8–9 (scans12–13): the source really has فلم تعلم ولم يحكم بشيء منها إلا بعد تعينها. Preserve the claim exactly; it remains a difficult question about determinacy and knowledge, not evidence for an invented manuscript emendation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": true,
      "manuscript_verified": false,
      "historical_print": {
        "siglum": "I1876",
        "printed_page": "8–9",
        "scan_index": "12–13",
        "reading": "فلم تعلم ولم يحكم بشيء منها إلا بعد تعينها",
        "status": "agreement",
        "source_pdf": "https://mc.dlib.nyu.edu/files/books/princeton_aco000264/princeton_aco000264_lo.pdf",
        "note": "Historic 1876 printing p8 end and p9 beginning preserve the difficult expression. This is a bounded print agreement; theological interpretation and manuscript text remain unresolved."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-033",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "وهي أبطن بطون أحكام الغيب الذاتي المغلوب والمسبوق بالرحمة الذاتية",
      "en": "These belong to the utmost inward depth of the judgments of the essential Unseen, which is preceded and overcome by essential mercy.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish hiddenness of rank from an event changing the Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-034",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "فلهذا أوجبت الرهبة والخشية المشار إليها في قوله تعالى : قُلْ ما كُنْتُ بِدْعاً مِنَ الرُّسُلِ وَما أَدْرِي ما يُفْعَلُ بِي وَلا بِكُمْ",
      "en": "They therefore occasion awe and fear, as indicated by 'Say: I am no novelty among messengers, and I do not know what will be done with me or with you' (Qurʾān 46:9).",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī's interpretation of the verse is an exegetical move; the verse alone does not prove his exact metaphysical thesis.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-035",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "ومن وجه أيضا يشير إليها قوله صلّى اللّه عليه وسلّم شفقا منها : « ليت ربّ محمد لم يخلق محمدا »",
      "en": "And from another aspect the saying attributed to the Prophet, expressing concern, 'Would that Muḥammad's Lord had not created Muḥammad', is taken as an allusion to them.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The Beirut editor explicitly reports no source found for this statement. Do not attribute it as authenticated hadith.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-036",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "مع أنه كان من حيث ما تعيّن من حاله صلّى اللّه عليه وسلّم في الحضرة العلمية واللّوح المحفوظ على بصيرة من ربّه",
      "en": "Yet concerning what had become determined of the Prophet's state in the Presence of Knowledge and the Preserved Tablet, he possessed insight from his Lord.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish this determined register from the unqualified Unseen of preceding clauses.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-037",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "حتى أنه كان يقول اعتمادا عليها ونظرا إليها : « آدم ومن دونه تحت لوائي يوم القيامة ولا فخر » ، « ولو كان موسى حيّا لما وسعه إلّا اتّباعي »",
      "en": "Thus in reliance on it he could say, 'Adam and those below him are under my banner on the Day of Resurrection, without boasting', and 'Were Moses alive, he would have no option but to follow me'.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Modern editor lists differing hadith collections for reports; do not treat every cited report as identically authenticated.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-038",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "وقوله : « أنا أول من يقرع باب الجنّة وأول من يفتح باب الشفاعة » ونحو ذلك من الإشارات في أحاديث صحاح",
      "en": "And 'I am the first to knock at the gate of Paradise and the first to open the gate of intercession', and comparable indications from reports he regards as sound.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Convey the authorial assessment 'aḥādīth ṣiḥāḥ' without issuing an independent authentication judgment.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-039",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "وعلى الحقيقة كثيرا ما يجرّه الدعاء والسؤال من الأمور والأحكام الغيبية إنما يكون من تلك الأمور والأحوال",
      "en": "In reality, much of what supplication and petition draw forth from unseen affairs and judgments pertains to those hidden things and states.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The relationship of supplication to hidden affairs needs comparison with his account of fixed divine knowledge.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-040",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "فإن ما عداها لا يتحصّل إلّا المكتوب الثابت المقسوم في الحضرة العلمية لا يزيد ولا ينقص",
      "en": "Apart from these, nothing occurs except what is written, established and allotted in the Presence of Knowledge, without increase or decrease.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Sets up tension with 'not known until they occur'; do not resolve by paraphrase.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-041",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "ثم إنه قد عبّر بعض العلماء باللّه الواقفين على حضراته الكلية عن بعض اعتبارات الواحدية المندرجة فيها في الرتبة الأولى بالشؤون الذاتية",
      "en": "Some knowers of God, acquainted with His universal Presences, have called certain considerations of oneness, included in it at the first rank, 'essential affairs' (shuʾūn dhātiyya).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Source begins an extended treatment continued p24.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-042",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "وهي التي تظهر في الرتبة الثانية وما تحتها من المراتب بصور الحقائق المتبوعة وبعض صفاتها التابعة التي لها الثبات ، نحو الحياة والعلم",
      "en": "These appear at the second and lower ranks as principal realities and certain enduring concomitant attributes, such as life and knowledge.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Thubūt of principal realities versus dependent qualities.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR002-043",
      "page": 23,
      "ar": "وبصور أمور كائنة ثابتة نحو الذوات والجواهر ، وصفة بقائها وثباتها اعتبارا لبعض",
      "en": "And as enduring beings such as individual entities and substances, and as properties of their continuance and stability, considered with respect to some—",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Sentence continues in p24; do not present as complete.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar",
      "grade": "printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    }
  ],

window.FARGHANI_CRITICAL_PASS_003={

  "records": [

      "disposition": "revise",

      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-002",
      "page": 24,
      "ar": "وعبّر بعضهم عن بعضها بالأحوال الذاتية",
      "en": "Some have designated certain of these as essential states (aḥwāl dhātiyya).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "'Some' is the author's attribution; he is reporting a usage, not necessarily establishing a universal technical definition.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-003",
      "page": 24,
      "ar": "كالقبض والبسط ، والرضا والغضب ، وجميع الأعراض التي لا تدوم",
      "en": "such as contraction and expansion, satisfaction and anger, and all accidents that do not endure.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The examples concern appearances and transformations in manifestation; do not infer accidental change in the divine Essence itself.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-004",
      "page": 24,
      "ar": "تذكر تحوّل الحق تعالى وتقدّس من صورة لم يعرفها قوم في الآخرة إلى صورة يعرفونه بها",
      "en": "Recall the Real's appearing, in the hereafter, in a form they do not recognize, then in a form in which they recognize Him.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The source ties the example to a transmitted report and recognitional difference, not an unqualified doctrine of bodily change.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-005",
      "page": 24,
      "ar": "اعلم أن أوّل اعتبار وتعيّن تعيّن من الغيب هذه الوحدة التي انتشأت منها الأحدية والواحدية",
      "en": "Know that the first consideration and determination issuing from the Unseen is the unity from which aḥadiyya and wāḥidiyya arise.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not identify first determination as already the differentiated second rank; aḥadiyya and wāḥidiyya are two respects of unity.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-006",
      "page": 24,
      "ar": "فظلّت برزخا جامعا بينهما",
      "en": "It remains an intermediary (barzakh) encompassing both.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Barzakh here denotes a comprehensive mediating respect, not a third material realm inserted between two divine substances.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-007",
      "page": 24,
      "ar": "كحقيقة المحبة المنتشئة منها المحبّية والمحبوبية وكونها جامعة بينهما ورافعة بينهما موحّدة إياهما",
      "en": "like the reality of love, from which loving and being beloved arise; it encompasses both, removes what divides them, and unites them.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "The printed wording ورافعة بينهما is compressed: a missing/elliptical object after rāfiʿa may underlie 'removes what divides them.' Maintain provisional reading; no manuscript emendation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-008",
      "page": 24,
      "ar": "هذا الاعتبار والتعيّن الأول الذي هو عين الذات بحكم البرزخية والجمعية المذكورة",
      "en": "this first consideration and determination, which is identical with the Essence under the stated consideration of intermediacy and comprehensiveness.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Identity is predicated 'under the consideration of' (bi-ḥukm), not a claim of a second additional divine entity.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-009",
      "page": 24,
      "ar": "فكانت هذه القابلية أصل كل قابلية وفاعلية أيضا",
      "en": "This receptivity is the root of every receptivity and also of all activity.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Qābiliyya and fāʿiliyya are correlated active/receptive principial considerations, not created passivity and action.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/24?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-010",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "والأسمائي المتعلّق بذلك الظهور حديثا وإخبارا نزيها بحرف نزيه وحداني هو عين الذات",
      "en": "and name-related perfection concerning that manifestation: an immaterial discourse and informing through a single transcendent 'letter' identical with the Essence.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Sentence continues p.24. 'Letter' is expressly non-corporeal; not a phonetic sound or a separate divine component.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-011",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "متضمّن جميع المعاني كلّياتها وجزئياتها",
      "en": "containing all meanings, universal and particular.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Meaningful totality is predicated of the inward unitary letter before differentiation at articulated levels.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-012",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "في سابع رتب أبطن الكلام",
      "en": "at the seventh rank of speech's inward depths.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The ordinal counts inward from the external, articulated expression; do not conflate the seven 'inward depths' with seven historical stages.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-013",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "إن للقرآن ظهرا وبطنّا ، وفي بطنه بطن إلى سبعة أبطن",
      "en": "The Qurʾān has an outward and an inward, and within its inward a further inward, to seven inward depths.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Reported saying appears in Farghānī's authorial text, but the modern editor states this exact wording was not located. Do not mark as authenticated prophetic ḥadīth.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-014",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "كما يتحدّث أحدنا في نفسه بلا واسطة حرف وصوت ظاهر",
      "en": "as one of us speaks inwardly without an outward letter or audible sound.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī explicitly says the comparison is analogical and excludes physical utterance.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-015",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "قابلية ميل الذات بالسماع والإصغاء إلى ذلك الحديث هي أثر تلك المحبّة في الحقيقة",
      "en": "the Essence's receptive inclination toward hearing and listening to this discourse is in truth the effect of that love.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Inclination, love, and hearing here name principial relational considerations, not change caused by lack in the Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-016",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "ملاحظة الذات نور جمالها الذي هو عين واحديتها وظهورها وظهور اعتباراتها",
      "en": "the Essence's beholding the light of its beauty, which is identical with its oneness, manifestation, and manifestation of its considerations.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep the repeated 'manifestation' rather than flattening the subject–object relation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-017",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "ليميط خمار غلبة حكم جلال الغيب والبطون عن الجمال والظهور",
      "en": "so as to remove the veil of the preponderance of the majesty of the Unseen and hiddenness from beauty and manifestation.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The digital source writes ليميط without vocalization; the 'veil' is figurative and must not be literalized.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-018",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "بتغليب الرحمة وسبقها على أنها غايات حكم الغضب عارجا لا نازلا",
      "en": "through the preponderance and precedence of mercy, in contrast to the furthest limit of the judgment called anger, upward rather than downward.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "I1876 p10 / scan14 now confirms the bounded concluding phrase حكم الغضب عارجا لا نازلا. This historic PRINT agreement does not decide the syntactic attachment of على أنها غايات and does not justify smoothing the whole sentence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": true,
      "manuscript_verified": false,
      "historical_print": {
