        "scan_index": 14,
        "reading": "حكم الغضب عارجا لا نازلا",
        "status": "agreement",
        "source_pdf": "https://mc.dlib.nyu.edu/files/books/princeton_aco000264/princeton_aco000264_lo.pdf",
        "note": "Narrow ending contrast is photographed in I1876 p10/scan14 and B2007 p25. Earlier على أنها غايات remains syntactically unsettled."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-019",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "فإن الظهور واللاظهور كان في حكم اقتضاء هذه القابلية الأولية على السواء",
      "en": "For manifestation and non-manifestation stood on an equal footing with respect to what this primordial receptivity entails.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This is a logical/principial contrast, not a chronological episode when God lacked manifestation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-020",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "فوجدت الذات نفسها بهذا التجلّي الذي هو عين العلم والشهود بحكم مرآته المذكورة",
      "en": "The Essence found itself through this self-disclosure, which itself is knowledge and witnessing, under the consideration of the mirror already mentioned.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "'Mirror' is identical with Essence under a respect, not a second existing object.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-021",
      "page": 25,
      "ar": "فإن القول عبارة عن نفس منبعث من باطن المتنفّس",
      "en": "Speech is a breath issuing from the inward aspect of the one breathing.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Analogical lexical analysis of 'speech' and 'breath': do not transfer corporeal exhalation to the Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-022",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "والسمع هو قبول ذلك المعنى ظاهرا أو باطنا",
      "en": "Hearing is the reception of that meaning, outwardly or inwardly.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Defines a faculty relative to meaning and its manifestation, not exclusively bodily audition.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-023",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "والبصر هو قبول ظهوره بما هو ظاهر من القابل منه",
      "en": "Sight is receptivity to the meaning's appearing in whatever becomes manifest to its recipient.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The printed Arabic is syntactically dense. The object is the appearance of the meaning; further historical witnesses may refine referents.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-024",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "والقوة تمكن مما يطلب ظهوره",
      "en": "Power is the capacity for the realization of what seeks to appear.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Quwwa is explicitly defined by the intended appearance, not bare mechanical force.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-025",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "فلفظ وكلي بي لسان محدث",
      "en": "My utterance—and my whole being is a tongue speaking through me.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "First Ibn al-Fāriḍ hemistich in Beirut. Collateral poetic reading: B2007 p26 gives ولحظ ... عين لعبرة, whereas a separate published Tāʾiyya in the Qayṣarī commentary (verse 545) and Wikisource read لعبرتي. These are parallel poem texts, NOT independent Farghānī manuscript witnesses.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-026",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "معاني صفات ما ورى اللّبس أثبتت",
      "en": "The meanings of the attributes were established beyond their clothing in multiplicity.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Lubs ('putting on' or 'clothing') is the metaphor for differentiation of what is not yet distinct at the first rank; previous 'beyond the clothing of appearance' under-specified this.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-027",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "صفات متغايرة في الرتبة الثانية بحسبها وحكمها",
      "en": "attributes distinct from one another at the second rank according to that rank's law.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Explicit differentiation occurs at second rank; avoid transferring independent multiplicity to the Essence at first rank.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-028",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "بعد أن لم تكن في تلك الرتبة الأولى صفات ، بل اعتبارات غير متميّزة ولا متغايرة فيها",
      "en": "though at that first rank they were not attributes but considerations neither distinct nor mutually divergent.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Critical distinction: shūʾūn/considerations at first rank vs distinct attributes at second rank.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-029",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "وباطنها الأول : أن تكون هذه الصفات مضافة إلى نفسه",
      "en": "The first inward level is the attribution of these qualities to the individual's soul.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The 'first inward' degree follows their outward manifestation in bodily faculties, and so is not the initial divine self-disclosure.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-030",
      "page": 26,
      "ar": "بظاهر العقل المعيشي المقيّد بأمور دنيوية",
      "en": "by the outward appearance of practical intellect restricted to worldly concerns.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "ʿAql maʿīshī is livelihood-oriented intellect; 'practical intellect' here is contextual and must not be mistaken for the whole Aristotelian practical intellect.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-031",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "وباطنها الثاني : ما يضاف إلى نفسه من حيث عقله المنوّر بنور الشرع والإيمان باللّه",
      "en": "The second inward level pertains to the soul insofar as its intellect is illuminated by revealed law and faith in God.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Law and faith illuminate intellect, enabling reorientation from world to afterlife; do not collapse into generic philosophical insight.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-032",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "وباطنها الثالث : ما يضاف منها إلى روحه الروحانية المجرّدة",
      "en": "The third inward level pertains to the individual's immaterial spiritual spirit.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Preserve the doubled expression rūḥuhu al-rūḥāniyya; it differentiates this level from ordinary psychical faculty.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-033",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "الثابت تعيّنها وتميّزها في عالم الأرواح واللّوح المحفوظ",
      "en": "whose determination and distinctness are established in the world of spirits and the Preserved Tablet.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "That distinctness belongs to a cosmic level; no reason to infer independent divine existence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-034",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "وباطنها الرابع : ما يضاف منها إلى الوجود العيني المضاف إلى حقيقة الإنسانية",
      "en": "The fourth inward level concerns concrete existence as related to the reality of humanity.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "ʿAynī is actual/concrete rather than 'eye-like'; at this level the same senses are interpreted as disclosures of existential reality.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-035",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "المقيّد بها والمظهر لأحكامها في مراتب الكون روحا ومثالا وحسّا",
      "en": "delimited by it and manifesting its rulings in cosmic ranks as spirit, image, and sense.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The three modalities remain distinguishable even when a single principle is at issue.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-036",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "وباطنها الخامس : ما يضاف منها إلى قلبه القابل التجلّي الوجود الباطني فيه",
      "en": "The fifth inward level relates to the heart receptive to the disclosure of inward existence within it.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "This is the heart's capacity of reception, not a claim of spatial containment of God.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-037",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "وسعني قلب عبدي",
      "en": "The heart of My servant encompassed Me.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī cites a well-known spiritual saying; its role in his argument does not authenticate it as a prophetic or divine ḥadīth.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-038",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "وباطنها السادس : ما يضاف منها إلى الوجود المطلق الرحمني الجمعي",
      "en": "The sixth inward level relates to comprehensive, merciful, nondelimited existence.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "'Nondelimited' here belongs to the specific sixth-level perspective, not automatically identical with absolutely unconditioned Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-039",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "الظاهر في مرتبة البرزخية الثانية بحكم فياضيته التي عمّ كل شيء",
      "en": "appearing at the second intermediary rank through its effusiveness, which extends to all things.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Crucial distinction between first determination and second intermediary, against prematurely equating all references to barzakh.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-040",
      "page": 27,
      "ar": "وسابع أبطنها : هي هذه المعاني الكائنة في الرتبة الأولى متوحّدة العين متميّزة الحكم بالنسبة",
      "en": "Their seventh inward depth consists of the meanings at the first rank, one in reality and distinguished in ruling only relationally.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Unity of reality and relative distinction of ḥukm, not numerical identity of faculties at their empirical level.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-041",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "ومنهم : من انفتح له باب ثالث وهو من خواصّهم",
      "en": "Some have the third gate opened: they are among the elect of the believers.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The ascent concerns differential openings of inward capacities, not simply social class.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-042",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "ومنهم : من انفتح له باب رابع ، وهو من أهل بداية مقام الولاية والإحسان",
      "en": "Some have the fourth opened: these are at the beginning of the station of sainthood and excellence (iḥsān).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep iḥsān as technical spiritual term.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-043",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "ومنهم : من انفتح له باب سادس ، وهو من أهل النهايات ، وهم الكمّل والأفراد",
      "en": "Others have the sixth opened: they are among those of the final stations, the perfected and the singular ones.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "al-Afrād is a technical Sufi category; do not paraphrase as merely 'individuals'.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-044",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "ولا تنفتح خوخة من الباب السابع لأحد إلّا لصاحب الإرث المحمدي",
      "en": "Not even a small aperture of the seventh gate opens to anyone except one who bears the Muhammadan inheritance.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The aperture/inheritance distinction qualifies the following uniqueness statement; the English should not exclude inheritors absolutely.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-045",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "فإن البطن السابع مختصّ به صلى اللّه عليه وسلم",
      "en": "for the seventh inward depth belongs particularly to him—God bless him and grant him peace.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "A Muhammadan inheritor's partial opening differs from the station's proper privilege; do not construe the sentences as a flat contradiction.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-046",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "وذلك أصل الحياة والحيّ وباطنه",
      "en": "that is the origin and inward reality of life and the Living.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī derives attribute concepts as principial considerations; distinction from later attributes remains essential.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-047",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "ومن حيث الملاحظة متضمّن إحساسا بسريان ذلك الكمال في تفاصيل اعتبارات الواحدية",
      "en": "insofar as it is contemplation, it includes awareness of that perfection's diffusion through the detailed considerations of oneness.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Here knowledge is indexed to wāḥidiyya and detailed considerations, not bare aḥadiyya.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-048",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "ومن حيث ذلك الميل بالسماع والإصغاء تقتضي اعتبارا هو أصل الإرادة",
      "en": "insofar as it involves inclining in hearing and attentive listening, it entails a consideration underlying will.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not infer psychological appetite from the term 'inclination'.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-049",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "وحكم تحقيق تفصيل الكمال ، وتحصيل شرائطه متضمّن حقيقة الجود بإفاضة الوجود على حقيقة كل موجود",
      "en": "The realization of perfection in detail, with its conditions, entails the reality of generosity through the bestowal of existence on the reality of each existent.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The 'generosity' / 'effusion of existence' linkage is explicit. This is not automatically Guénon's account of principial possibility.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-050",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "ورعاية العدالة في القوابل بين الوجوب والاستحالة",
      "en": "and the preservation of due proportion among receptivities between necessity and impossibility.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Justice is the appropriate distribution of ranks according to modalities of receptivity.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-051",
      "page": 28,
      "ar": "من حيث تحقّقها في حاق البرزخية الثانية",
      "en": "insofar as these are realized at the very core of the second intermediacy.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "Attachment of the plural feminine pronoun and transition from the first to second barzakh require punctuation and witness checking. Avoid merging the two levels.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/28?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-052",
      "page": 29,
      "ar": "وباطن كل حقيقة إلهية وكونيّة",
      "en": "the inward reality of every divine and cosmic reality.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Continuation of 'Reality of Realities' from p28; page break must not sever the syntax.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-053",
      "page": 29,
      "ar": "وهو سار بكليته فيه بحيث يكون في الإلهيّة إلهيّا ، وفي الكونية كونيّا",
      "en": "it permeates each in its entirety, such that it is divine in divine realities and cosmic in cosmic realities.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not translate pervasion as literal local diffusion or confusion of creator and creation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-054",
      "page": 29,
      "ar": "وسمّاه بعضهم البرزخ الأكبر الجامع لجميع البرازخ وأصلها الساري فيها",
      "en": "Some call it the Supreme Intermediary, encompassing all intermediaries and being their pervasive principle.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Attribution is to 'some'; not an equation automatically accepted across all Akbarī authors.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-055",
      "page": 29,
      "ar": "وكنى عنه الشرع بمقام أو أدنى ، فإنه باطن مقام قاب قوسين",
      "en": "Revelation alludes to it through the station of 'or nearer,' the inward reality of 'the distance of two bows'.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Qurʾān 53:9 is interpreted through Farghānī's metaphysical hermeneutics; distinguish Qurʾānic wording from that interpretation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR003-056",
      "page": 29,
      "ar": "أي قرب قوسي الوحدة والكثرة ، أو قل القابلية والفاعلية ، أو قل قوسي الوجوب والإمكان",
      "en": "that is, the two bows of unity and multiplicity, or receptivity and activity, or necessity and possibility.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Three alternative relational axes are illustrative of a comprehensive intermediary, not interchangeable logical predicates in every context.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar",

    {

      "page": 29,
      "ar": "ثم إن هذا التجلّي الأول المذكور كما قلنا يتضمّن الكمال الذي حقيقته حصول ما ينبغي على نحو ما ينبغي",
      "en": "The first self-disclosure includes perfection, whose reality is the obtaining of what ought to be, as it ought to be.",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-002",
      "page": 30,
      "ar": "ومن كون الكلّ عينا واحدا بالنسبة إلى شهود الحقّ الواحد الأحد",
      "en": "and to everything's being one reality relative to the witnessing of the One Real in unicity.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The distinction is between the cosmos' distinct witnessing and a principial witnessing; not an assertion that created entities are unqualifiedly God.",

      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-003",
      "page": 30,
      "ar": "وأمّا القسم الثاني من الكمال ، فهو الكمال الأسمائي المفصّل",
      "en": "The second division of perfection is differentiated perfection through the names.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Marks the contrast with the first, essentially comprehensive perfection.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-004",
      "page": 30,
      "ar": "وذلك يكون بشرط شيء ، بل أشياء",
      "en": "This occurs on condition of something—indeed, several conditions.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Name-related perfection is conditioned by modes and loci; essential perfection is not.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-005",
      "page": 30,
      "ar": "بشرط أن يكون ذلك الشهود من حيث مظهر شأن كلّي جامع جميع أفرادها بالفعل وهو الإنسان الكامل الحقيقي",
      "en": "provided that witnessing occurs through a manifestation of a universal affair encompassing its members in actuality: the real Perfect Human.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Do not generalize 'the Perfect Human' as merely the sum of humans. The Arabic emphasizes universality and actuality.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-006",
      "page": 30,
      "ar": "واعتبار العلم الذي حقيقته ظهور عين العين بحيث يكون أثر الظاهر حاصلا فيمن ظهر له",
      "en": "and the consideration of knowledge, whose reality is the appearance of the very reality, so that its effect is present in the one to whom it appears.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "ʿAyn occurs twice in different syntactic relations; a flat 'object appears' translation hides the self-manifestation terminology.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-007",
      "page": 30,
      "ar": "واعتبار الوجود الذي حقيقته ما به وجدان العين نفسه في نفسه أو في غيره",
      "en": "and existence, by which a reality finds itself in itself or in another.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep the technical verbal link wujūd / wijdān explicit.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-008",
      "page": 30,
      "ar": "واعتبار النور الذي هو الكاشف للمستور واعتبار الشهود الذي هو الحضور مع المشهود",
      "en": "light as that which discloses the concealed, and witnessing as presence with the witnessed.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "These are farghānī's explicit definitions under a particular consideration, not generic modern definitions.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-009",
      "page": 30,
      "ar": "فإن المحل له أثر لازم وحكم جازم فيما قام به وثبت فيه",
      "en": "A locus exercises an inescapable effect and decisive judgment on whatever is established in it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Effect of the locus explains form of manifestation without making the absolute itself multiply substantially.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-010",
      "page": 31,
      "ar": "لون الماءلون إنائه",
      "en": "The color of water is the color of its vessel.",
      "disposition": "corrected",
      "critical_note": "Digital source concatenates الماءلون without a separator; space 'الماء لون' is a typographic normalization supported by the familiar maxim, NOT a manuscript variant.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-011",
      "page": 31,
      "ar": "مراتب الكون لا يظهر فيها إلّا الخلق ، ومراتب الحق لا يظهر فيها إلّا الحق",
      "en": "In the ranks of the cosmos only creation appears; in the ranks of the Real only the Real appears.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Preserve the two predicative standpoints; avoid denying divine causal action in the cosmos.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-012",
      "page": 31,
      "ar": "لا يعرف ولا يشهد ولا يرى الحقّ إلا هو أو به",
      "en": "None knows, witnesses, or sees the Real except the Real Himself or through Him.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Alternative 'through Him' is in the Arabic and indispensable for the experiential register.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-013",
      "page": 31,
      "ar": "فكان متعلّقا بمعلوم واحد",
      "en": "It thus relates to one thing known.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Knowledge first rank indexed to one object while the second rank has articulated respects.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-014",
      "page": 31,
      "ar": "فكان لفظه حينئذ متعدّيا بحسب هذه المرتبة الأولى ، بمفعول واحد",
      "en": "Its expression, at this first rank, is transitive to one object.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī makes an unusual verbal-transitivity analogy: it is his own grammatical illustration, not a general rule about the verb ʿalima; explicate with caution.",

      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-015",
      "page": 31,
      "ar": "فكان للعلم بحسب انتهاء المرتبة الثانية وحكم معلوماته فيها كثرة حقيقية ووحدة نسبية مجموعية",
      "en": "Knowledge at the completion of the second rank has real multiplicity and a collective relative unity in respect of its known contents.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not ascribe multiplicity to the undelimited Essence without the second-rank qualifier.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-016",
      "page": 31,
      "ar": "فله حينئذ وحدة حقيقية وكثرة نسبيّة",
      "en": "It therefore has real unity and relational multiplicity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Key register: unity of wujūd as manifested names, versus real diversity in cosmic modes.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-017",
      "page": 31,
      "ar": "فبالنظر إلى عين الوجود ونفس التعيّن هو عين الذات",
      "en": "As regards the reality of existence and determination itself, it is identical with the Essence.",

      "critical_note": "Refers to determination qua its intrinsic reality, not every limited predicate.",

      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-018",
      "page": 32,
      "ar": "وأمّا النوع الثاني من الوجود الظاهر من حيث ما هو منها مجلى الظهور للكون",
      "en": "The second mode of manifest existence concerns a locus of manifestation of the cosmos.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep distinct from second rank as the Real's name-bearing manifestation.",

      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/32?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",

    },

      "ar": "كمرتبة الأرواح والمثال والحسّ",
      "en": "such as the rank of spirits, of imaginal form, and of sense.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Not three chronological stages; three ontological levels of manifestation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/32?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-020",
      "page": 32,
      "ar": "فالوجود حينئذ معناه وحقيقته ما به وجدان صورة كل تعين منه نفسها ومثلها موجودا",
      "en": "Existence here is what allows the form of each determination to find itself and its like as existent.",
      "disposition": "retain",

      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-021",
      "page": 32,
      "ar": "فالإيجاد والخلق على هذا ما هو إلّا إعطاء الموجد الخالق تعالى وتقدّس للحقائق الكونيّة ما به وجدانها",
      "en": "On this account creating consists in the Creator's granting cosmic realities that by which they find themselves.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not reduce this to an assertion of autonomous creative causality.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/32?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-022",
      "page": 32,
      "ar": "وهي أمور ونسب عدمية لا وجود لها في الخارج ولا نفسها",
      "en": "These loci are privative affairs and relations possessing no external existence in themselves.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The negative is aimed at ranks as intelligible relations, not a denial that their effects or loci of appearance are experienced.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/32?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-023",
      "page": 32,
      "ar": "فانظر أثرا لمعدوم وإن كان عدميّته بنسبة ووجه ما في عين الوجود",
      "en": "Observe the effect of something non-existent—though non-existence is itself relative and in one respect—within the reality of existence.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This is not the claim that absolute nothingness is an efficient cause; Farghānī expressly qualifies the privation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/32?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-024",
      "page": 32,
      "ar": "ولم تكن قابلة لتجلّي قابل للكثرة ، وإن كانت نسبية",
      "en": "It could not receive a disclosure admitting plurality, even merely relational plurality.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The first determination's capacity is constrained by the pure-unicity consideration.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/32?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-025",
      "page": 32,
      "ar": "ولما كانت المحبة الأصلية المعبّر عنها « بأحببت » حاملة لهذا التجلّي الأول",
      "en": "Original love, expressed by 'I loved', carried this first disclosure.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Continuation p33 must be kept as one argument.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/32?lang=ar",

      "page": 33,
      "ar": "ولم يصادف توجّهه ذلك محلّا قابلا لما توجه له",
      "en": "but its orientation did not encounter a locus receptive to what it intended.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Continuation from p32; intended aim is differentiated name-perfection.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/33?lang=ar",

      "id": "CR004-027",
      "page": 33,
      "ar": "رجع بقوّة ذلك التوجّه الشوقي والميل العشقي إلى أصله",
      "en": "It returned to its source by the force of its yearning orientation and loving inclination.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "'Return' is a rank-based principial metaphor, not temporal divine movement.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/33?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-028",
      "page": 33,
      "ar": "حكم الظهور المعبّر عنه بالرحمة الذاتية",
      "en": "the judgment of manifestation, expressed as essential mercy.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish mercy here from a psychological affect and later manifested divine name.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/33?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-029",
      "page": 33,
      "ar": "فعاد ذلك التجلّي ظاهرا معيّنا في عون هذا بقوة المحبة الأصلية اللازمة له",
      "en": "The disclosure appeared again as determined [the phrase 'في عون هذا' remains uncertain], through its inseparable original love.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "Original-resolution I1876 printed p16 (scan20) confirms في عون هذا exactly as in Beirut p33; proposed عين is NOT supported by these two prints. Maintain diplomatic reading, with grammatical argument and early manuscripts still unresolved. Do not call a print agreement a definitive authorial lemma.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/33?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": true,
      "manuscript_verified": false,
      "historical_print": {
        "siglum": "I1876",
        "printed_page": 16,
        "scan_index": 20,
        "reading": "في عون هذا",
        "status": "agreement",
        "source_pdf": "https://mc.dlib.nyu.edu/files/books/princeton_aco000264/princeton_aco000264_lo.pdf",
        "hires": true,
        "photo_url": "https://github.com/gnamma-research/booksite/blob/master/texts/al-farghani/muntaha-al-madarik/facsimile-I1876-hires/images/scan_0020.jpg",
        "note": "The original-resolution I1876 p16 prints في عون هذا, agreeing with Beirut p33. This rejects an unsupported conjectural عين at print level but does not resolve syntactic reference or prove a medieval MS reading."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-030",
      "page": 33,
      "ar": "وذلك التعيّن هو القابل الثاني الجامع بين طرفي حكم الإجمال والوحدة ، ومقابلهما الذي هو التفصيل والكثرة",
      "en": "That determination is the second receptive principle combining the poles of comprehensive unity and differentiated multiplicity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Qābil thānī is a determinate receptive principle, not a second eternal substance.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/33?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-031",
      "page": 33,
      "ar": "من تلك الحضرة الخفائية الإطلاقية أثرا من ظلمة غيبه وإطلاق أزليّته",
      "en": "from that concealed, undelimited Presence a trace of the darkness of hiddenness and nondelimitation of pre-eternity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Trace of hiddenness describes a logical/ontological condition of manifestation, not physical material.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/33?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-032",
      "page": 33,
      "ar": "وجميع الأسماء الإلهيّة المنتمي إليها التأثير والفعل ، مضافة إلى عين التجلّي الثاني",
      "en": "all the divine names, to which efficacy and action pertain, are attributed to the second self-disclosure.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Critical complement to cosmic realities as receptive correlates.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/33?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-033",
      "page": 33,
      "ar": "بحسب ما هو كل واحد عليه من هذه المعلومات ، لا بحسب العلم",
      "en": "according to what each known thing is, not according to [a restriction in] knowledge.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Important qualification for the earlier 'real multiplicity' of objects known; do not make divine knowledge essentially variable.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/33?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-034",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "ومراتب الاعتدالات الموجودة فيها المركّبات المسماة بالمولدات",
      "en": "the ranks of equilibria in which the composites called generated things arise.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Muwalladāt refers to generated beings; preserve Aristotelian/cosmological technical register.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-035",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "ثم إن هذا التعيّن الثاني النفسي المذكور سمي بأسماء كثيرة بحسب اعتبارات ثابتة فيه مع توحد عينه",
      "en": "This second, breath-like determination has many names corresponding to considerations within it while remaining one in reality.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Nafasī is breath-related, not psychical nafsī; must disambiguate transliteration.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-036",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "سمّي بمرتبة الألوهية",
      "en": "It is called the rank of divinity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "One of multiple descriptions of a single second determination, not a new rank.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-037",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "سمّي بعالم المعاني",
      "en": "It is called the world of meanings.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Same referent, different explanatory iʿtibār.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-038",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "سمّي بحضرة الارتسام",
      "en": "It is called the Presence of Inscription.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Rasm/irtisām indicates the inscription or impression of relative multiplicity; avoid an unglossed transliteration.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-039",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "سمّي بحضرة العلم الأزلي",
      "en": "It is called the Presence of Eternal Knowledge.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Explicitly named by knowledge's encompassing attachment to plurality, not identification with independent entities.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-040",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "سمّي بالحضرة العملية",
      "en": "It is called the Cloud-like Presence (al-ḥaḍra al-ʿamāʾiyya).",
      "disposition": "corrected",
      "critical_note": "CORRECTED PRINT-TO-PRINT 9 Nov 2026: historical I1876 p18 (scan22) visibly reads الحضرة العمائية where B2007 p34 has الحضرة العملية. B2007 p36 agrees with I1876 in wording. Adopt Cloud-like Presence; manuscripts still uncollated.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": true,
      "manuscript_verified": false,
      "edited_ar": "سمّي بالحضرة العمائية",
      "i1876_ar": "سمّي بالحضرة العمائية",
      "print_locus": "I1876 printed p18, scan 22",
      "evidence_note": "I1876 p18/scan22 directly inspected: الحضرة العمائية; B2007 p34 printed الرقمية: الحضرة العملية. Adopt historical print lemma, record B2007 in apparatus; no manuscript variant verified.",
      "historical_print": {
        "siglum": "I1876",
        "printed_page": 18,
        "scan_index": 22,
        "reading": "سمّي بالحضرة العمائية",
        "status": "historical-print-difference",
        "source_pdf": "https://mc.dlib.nyu.edu/files/books/princeton_aco000264/princeton_aco000264_lo.pdf",
        "note": "Photographed 1876 printing p18 reads العمائية; Beirut B2007 p34 has العملية. Adopt 'Cloud-like Presence'. This is not a manuscript variant."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-041",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "سمّي بالحقيقة الإنسانية الكمالية",
      "en": "It is called the Perfectional Human Reality.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Not just 'human nature': here specifically the comprehensive intermediate human reality.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-042",
      "page": 34,
      "ar": "سمي المتوسّط مرتبة الإمكان",
      "en": "The intermediate category is called the rank of possibility.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Possibility lies conceptually between necessary and impossible manifestation, distinct from psychological probabilities.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/34?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-043",
      "page": 35,
      "ar": "وحدة وكثرة وبرزخ فاصل وجامع بينهما",
      "en": "These possess unity, multiplicity, and an intermediary both separating and comprehending them.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The threefold structure must not be conflated with three distinct substances.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/35?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-044",
      "page": 35,
      "ar": "أمّا وحدته ، فمضافة إلى التجلّي الثاني المذكور الذي نسبته إلى الأحدية الذاتية أقوى",
      "en": "Its unity belongs to the second disclosure, whose relation to essential unicity is stronger.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The second level retains an aspect of aḥadiyya despite differentiation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/35?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-045",
      "page": 35,
      "ar": "فانتشاء الكثرة النسبية الأسمائية السلبية والثبوتية منه",
      "en": "Thus relational multiplicity of negative and affirmative names proceeds from it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep distinction between negative names and affirmative predicates.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/35?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-046",
      "page": 35,
      "ar": "والسلام المنفي عنه تنازع ظهور الصفات",
      "en": "The name Peace excludes contest among the manifestations of the attributes.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Peace is a technical divine name, not merely absence of conflict in general.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/35?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-047",
      "page": 35,
      "ar": "وأما طرف كثرة هذا التعين الثاني ، فظهر للواحدية الذاتية لكن بسراية أثر الأحدية فيه",
      "en": "Its multiplicity manifests essential oneness while bearing the permeating effect of unicity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Dual perspective explains simultaneous unity and multiplicity without incoherence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/35?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-048",
      "page": 35,
      "ar": "وتميّزها فيه بعضها حقيقة وبعضها نسبيّة",
      "en": "and their distinctions within it are in some cases real, in others relational.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not universalize 'real distinction' to divine Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/35?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-049",
      "page": 35,
      "ar": "فإن رتبة تلك الوحدة متقدّمة على رتبة ما انتشأ منها من تلك الأعيان",
      "en": "The rank of the unity precedes the rank of the entities that proceed from it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Priority by rank, not temporal creation of a divine attribute.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/35?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-050",
      "page": 36,
      "ar": "الوحدة كالتجلّي أولا ، ثم كاسم اللّه والرحمن ، ثم كاسم الحيّ",
      "en": "Unity is exemplified first by the disclosure, then the names Allāh and al-Raḥmān, then the Living.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Ordering begins with the disclosure rather than a plurality of distinct gods.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/36?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-051",
      "page": 36,
      "ar": "فكل واحد مما ذكرنا مشتمل على أشخاصه وأنواعه المتفرّعة والمنتشئة منه",
      "en": "Each encompasses individuals and kinds that branch from it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Universal to particular derivation in knowledge and concrete manifestation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/36?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-052",
      "page": 36,
      "ar": "فإنما هي الحقيقة الإنسانية",
      "en": "That intermediary is the human reality.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Mixed grammatical gender al-barzakh ... hiya al-ḥaqīqa signals a predicate shift to feminine 'reality', not automatically a corrupt reading.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/36?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-053",
      "page": 36,
      "ar": "فمسمى بالحضرة العمائية",
      "en": "It is called the Cloud-like Presence (al-ḥaḍra al-ʿamāʾiyya).",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Important exact same referent as p34's variant-looking 'al-ḥaḍra al-ʿamaliyya'; do not silently harmonize the two.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/36?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-054",
      "page": 36,
      "ar": "فأسبقها تعيّنا وأشملها حكما حقيقة الحياة",
      "en": "The first determined and most comprehensive in rule is the reality of life.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This is a hierarchy of universal names, not chronological birth of divine life.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/36?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-055",
      "page": 36,
      "ar": "واسم الحيّ هو عين منبع الكمال الذي يستوعب كل كمال يليق به",
      "en": "The name the Living is the source of perfection embracing every perfection appropriate to it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish life at first determination from detailed naming at second rank.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/36?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR004-056",
      "page": 36,
      "ar": "والعلم في هذه المرتبة الثانية متعلق بمعلومات مفصّلة متميّزة",
      "en": "At the second rank knowledge relates to differentiated known things.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Index the doctrinal claim by rank; do not infer a new essence of divine knowledge.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/36?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    }
  ]
};

      "page": 37,
      "ar": "فكان المريد والإرادة داخلا في العلم ومنتشئا منه",
      "en": "Thus the Willing and willing are included in knowledge and arise from it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The order describes the derivation of divine-name meanings, not successive temporal events.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/37?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
