    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-002",
      "page": 37,
      "ar": "ولما كان القول حقيقته نفس منبعث من باطن المتنفّس متضمّن معنى يطلب ظهوره",
      "en": "Because speech is in its reality a breath arising from within a breather and bearing a meaning that seeks manifestation.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Here نفس is nafas (breath), established by المتنفس and مخارج, rather than nafs (soul). The unvocalized digital source does not distinguish them.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/37?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-003",
      "page": 37,
      "ar": "فكان لذلك داخلا في القول ومنبعثا ومتفرّعا منه",
      "en": "Therefore [power] is included in speech, originates from it and branches from it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The antecedent is القدرة, not الجود. Keep the page-wide referential sequence explicit.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/37?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-004",
      "page": 37,
      "ar": "وانبعاث بعضها من بعض نزولا ، واندراج بعضها في بعض عودا ورجوعا",
      "en": "Their emergence one from another in descent and their mutual inclusion on return and re-ascent.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The two directions are deliberately symmetrical; not merely a list of seven names.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/37?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-005",
      "page": 38,
      "ar": "( والقابل ) يجمعها من أن كل واحد منها من تعيّنات النفس الرحمني",
      "en": "The Receptive gathers them insofar as each is a determination of the Breath of the All-merciful.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "STAGE XV PHOTO UPDATE: I1876 printed p20 scan24 explicitly reads القابل, agreeing with the B2007 digitized Arabic on this disputed word. The earlier conjecture القائل is NOT witnessed in either printed source; retain القابل as the provisional edited lemma. Distinguish receptivity from the seven-name category القائل attested elsewhere. MSS uncollated.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/38?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": true,
      "manuscript_verified": false,
      "historical_print": {
        "printed_page": 20,
        "scan_index": 24,
        "status": "historic-print-agreement",
        "reading": "والقابل يجمعها",
        "note": "The noun القابل is present in the photographed 1876 printed page, despite internal parallel mentions of القائل elsewhere. See I1876-L11; no manuscript evidence.",
        "source_pdf": "https://mc.dlib.nyu.edu/files/books/princeton_aco000264/princeton_aco000264_lo.pdf",
        "original_image_url": "https://sites.dlib.nyu.edu/viewer/api/image/books/princeton_aco000264/24/full/2200,/0/default.jpg",
        "original_viewer_url": "https://sites.dlib.nyu.edu/viewer/books/princeton_aco000264/1?embed=1&lang=en",
        "archived_scan_sha256": "3bdb65963d2ba22687ee50071b08e7b1983796ec50642da2d021b30169d26d00",
        "linked_collation": "editorial/I1876_COLLATION_CUMULATIVE_005.json"
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-006",
      "page": 38,
      "ar": "( والقادر ) يشملها لصحة إضافة التمكّن من التأثير إلى كل واحد منها بأثر مختصّ به",
      "en": "The Powerful comprises them because a capacity to produce an effect special to each can be attributed to each.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Each name has an effect proper to its distinct reality.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/38?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-007",
      "page": 38,
      "ar": "أحدهما : اشتمال كل واحد منها على الباقي",
      "en": "First, each of them contains all the rest.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The first consideration is inclusive unity with a latent trace of difference.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/38?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-008",
      "page": 38,
      "ar": "تحقّق تميّزها وظهور أثر تختصّ بكل واحد منها",
      "en": "Their differentiation becomes established and an effect proper to each appears.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The inverse second consideration makes differentiation explicit but inclusion latent.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/38?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-009",
      "page": 39,
      "ar": "وحقائق الكمل الذين هم أولو العزم من الرّسل ثابتة ومندرجة فيها",
      "en": "The realities of the perfected messengers of firm resolve are fixed and included within it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "These are intelligible prophetic realities; do not suggest historical prophets literally merged into a person.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/39?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-010",
      "page": 39,
      "ar": "انتشأت من هذه الحقائق السبعة الأصلية الإلهية اثنان وتسعون حقيقة إلهيّة",
      "en": "From these seven original divine realities emerge ninety-two divine realities.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Seven plus ninety-two supplies the schematic ninety-nine; source is an internal metaphysical enumeration.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/39?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-011",
      "page": 39,
      "ar": "تسع وتسعون حقيقة تعيّنت بها الأسماء التسعة والتسعون",
      "en": "Ninety-nine realities by which the ninety-nine names become determined.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep realities distinct from the verbal expressions that designate the names (cf. p44).",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/39?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-012",
      "page": 39,
      "ar": "وهذه كلها تفصيل الحقيقة الكلّية المحمديّة الشاملة كلها",
      "en": "All these are differentiations of the universal Muhammadan reality which comprehends them all.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The universal's relation to particulars is the author's analogy; not physical distribution of a material substance.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/39?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-013",
      "page": 40,
      "ar": "فكل ما تنسب إليه الجمعية بينهما والمضاهاة لبرزخيّتهما ، ولو بنسبة ما كانت حقيقة إنسانية",
      "en": "Whatever has even some comprehensive likeness to the intermediacy between them constitutes a human reality.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Clarify that إنسانية denotes a metaphysical mode or reality, not merely belonging to the biological human species.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/40?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-014",
      "page": 40,
      "ar": "فكان ذلك الاسم ربّه ، فلا يأخذ إلّا منه ، ولا يعطي إلّا به",
      "en": "That name is its Lord: it receives only from it and gives only through it.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Here ربّه is lordship under a determining divine name, not an autonomous deity or identity of a created individual with the Divine Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/40?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-015",
      "page": 40,
      "ar": "وسمّيت أعيانا ثابتة لثبوتها في المرتبة الثانية لم تبرح منها",
      "en": "They are called fixed entities because they are established in the second rank and do not leave it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This says their fixity is in the second determination, not their physical subsistence as formed bodies.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/40?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-016",
      "page": 40,
      "ar": "ولم يظهر بالوجود العينيّ إلا لوازمها وأحكامها وعوارضها المتعلّقة بمراتب الكون",
      "en": "Only their concomitants, judgments and accidents connected to cosmic ranks appear in concrete existence.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Decisive distinction for fixed entities: do not say the fixed entities themselves migrate into concrete existence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/40?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-017",
      "page": 41,
      "ar": "والكل أمور نسبية معدومة لأنفسها لا وجود لها إلّا في علم العالم بها",
      "en": "All are relational matters, nonexistent in themselves, existing only in the knowledge of the one who knows them.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Read with the p40 account of quiddities; the statement concerns these determinate relational realities.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/41?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-018",
      "page": 41,
      "ar": "كان في عماء ما فوقه هواء ، وما تحته هواء",
      "en": "He was in ʿamāʾ; the following phrase concerning air above and below remains syntactically disputed.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "The Arabic phrase has competing interpretations: mā as negation (no air above/below) versus relative/interrogative or a variant without mā. Classical transmission itself varies. Do not silently impose one vocalization; see al-Tirmidhī 3109, Ibn Qutayba Taʾwīl mukhtalif al-ḥadīth, and critical hadith notes.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/41?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-019",
      "page": 41,
      "ar": "وهذا التجلّي الثاني الوجودي الظاهري العيني سار في هذه الحضرة العمائية",
      "en": "This second self-disclosure in outward, concrete existence pervades the Cloud-like Presence.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The second existential manifestation is an outward disclosure; avoid treating ʿamāʾ as a merely meteorological cloud.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/41?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-020",
      "page": 42,
      "ar": "إنما هي حقائق معنوية",
      "en": "They are intelligible realities.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The universal ranks are not literal spatial containers; the metaphor of loci of manifestation is qualified by معنوية.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/42?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-021",
      "page": 42,
      "ar": "قسم منسوب إلى الحق مختصّ به",
      "en": "A class ascribed exclusively to the Real.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "First of the three divisions of realities.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/42?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-022",
      "page": 42,
      "ar": "القسم الثاني : منسوب إلى الكون ؛ كالفقر والعدمية الذاتية والذلّة والإمكان",
      "en": "The second class belongs to the cosmos: poverty, intrinsic nonbeing, lowliness and possibility.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep فقر and إمكانية as distinct but related categories.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/42?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-023",
      "page": 42,
      "ar": "والقسم الثالث : ما ينسب إلى الحقّ بالأصالة ، وإلى الكون بتبعية إضافة الوجود إليه",
      "en": "The third is attributed primarily to the Real and secondarily to the cosmos by attribution of existence to it.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The same label (such as knowledge) can be predicated in differing modes; do not equate divine and creaturely possession.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/42?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-024",
      "page": 43,
      "ar": "فأمّا الكلّيات والمتبوعات ، فما برحت محصورة في مبدأ الحضرة العلمية والمرتبة الثانية",
      "en": "Universals and principal realities never depart from the source of the Presence of Knowledge, the second rank.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Principal intelligibles remain at their source; their concomitants manifest through spiritual, imaginal and sensible ranks.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/43?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-025",
      "page": 43,
      "ar": "وهذه المراتب الكلّية منحصرة في خمس مراتب : اثنتان منها منسوبتان إلى الحق ، وثلاث منسوبة إلى الكون ، وسادسها جامع بينها",
      "en": "The universal ranks number five—two attributed to the Real and three to the cosmos—and a sixth that unites them.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The count is FIVE plus a SIXTH comprehensive rank; do not collapse this into five equally homogeneous ranks.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/43?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-026",
      "page": 43,
      "ar": "وظهورها للعالم بها ، لا لأنفسها وأمثالها",
      "en": "They are manifest to the Knower of them, but not to themselves or their likes.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinct second-rank intelligibility from creaturely self-manifestation in later worlds.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/43?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-027",
      "page": 44,
      "ar": "وإنما هذه الألفاظ هي أسماء تلك الأسماء والدلالات عليها",
      "en": "These expressions are names of those names and indications pointing to them.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Names as ontological determinations are distinct from mutable linguistic signs; retain 'names of names' rather than calling the words the divine realities themselves.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/44?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-028",
      "page": 44,
      "ar": "وكلمة « تنكري » بالتركية متغيّرة متبدّلة ومختلفة",
      "en": "The Turkish expression 'Tengrī' too changes and differs.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The printed Arabic gives تنكري (a historical spelling of a Turkic divine designation). Retain the literal attested spelling and distinguish normalization from author's text.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/44?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-029",
      "page": 44,
      "ar": "أولها : أسماء الذات ، وثانيها : أسماء الصفات ، وثالثها : أسماء الأفعال",
      "en": "First are names of Essence; second, names of attributes; third, names of acts.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "These are three ontological degrees, distinguished in addition to the crosscutting negative/affirmative division.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/44?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-030",
      "page": 45,
      "ar": "فإن لم يتعدّ من ذلك التعيّن والاعتبار أثر إلى الغير في مرتبة من المراتب الكونية",
      "en": "If no effect of that determination and consideration passes to another at a cosmic rank.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This is the defining condition for names of attributes when the determination is not itself identical to Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/45?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-031",
      "page": 45,
      "ar": "وإن تعدّى أثر منه في مرتبة من مراتب الكون",
      "en": "But if an effect from it extends into one of the cosmic ranks.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Such names are names of acts, e.g., Creator, Originator, Fashioner.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/45?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-032",
      "page": 45,
      "ar": "وتجلّيه الصفاتي لم يحصل إلّا بالتجرّد عن جميع أحكام المراتب الكونية ومظاهرها",
      "en": "His attributive self-disclosure occurs only through divestment of all judgments and manifestations of cosmic ranks.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish modes of spiritual disclosure, not changing Divine Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/45?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-033",
      "page": 46,
      "ar": "فانحصرت أقسام الأسماء كلّها في ثمانية أبواب",
      "en": "All the classes of names are thereby confined to eight doors.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The preceding divisions are nested and overlapping; do not treat the eight as a flat mutually exclusive alphabetical list.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/46?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-034",
      "page": 46,
      "ar": "نوع ليس له صورة ظاهرة لفظيّة تدلّنا عليه هي اسمه",
      "en": "One kind has no outward verbal form that would indicate it to us as its name.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Retain distinction between an ontological name and a verbally available name.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/46?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-035",
      "page": 46,
      "ar": "أحدهما : مضمرات كلفظ هو وأنا ونحن",
      "en": "One kind consists of pronouns, such as 'He', 'I', and 'we'.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Context-specific speaker/person index is itself a modality of naming.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/46?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-036",
      "page": 46,
      "ar": "وجميع ما ذكرنا من الأسماء كلّها توقيفية على المذهب الصحيح",
      "en": "All the names discussed are, on the sound view, received by authoritative disclosure.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The author explicitly mentions revelation, sound unveiling and explicit divine communication; do not flatten his rule into a modern juristic consensus.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/46?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-037",
      "page": 47,
      "ar": "التي من أحصاها تعلّقا أو تخلّقا أو تحقّقا دخل الجنّة",
      "en": "Whoever enumerates them by connection, adopting their qualities, or realization enters Paradise.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Three distinct modalities of iḥṣāʾ. The overall hadith context is not itself proof that the triad is Prophetic wording; classify it as Farghānī's exegesis.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/47?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-038",
      "page": 47,
      "ar": "أمّا إحصاؤها تعلّقا ، فبأن يتطلّب آثار كل واحد منها في نفسه وبدنه",
      "en": "Enumeration through taʿalluq consists in seeking the effects of each name within one's soul and body.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The first mode combines tracing divine-name effects with appropriate responses of servanthood.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/47?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-039",
      "page": 47,
      "ar": "فيقابل كل واحد بما يليق من شكر أو صبر",
      "en": "He meets each with its fitting response of gratitude or patience.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The practical endpoint is moral and devotional responsiveness, not bare cataloguing.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/47?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-040",
      "page": 47,
      "ar": "وأمّا إحصاؤها تخلّقا ، فبتطلّع الروح الروحانية إلى حقائق هذه الأسماء",
      "en": "Enumeration through takhalluq is the spiritual spirit's aspiration toward the realities of these names.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The subsequent 'Take on God's character traits' dictum requires separate hadith/source criticism; preserve it as an attributed saying, not an established Prophetic proof.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/47?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-041",
      "page": 50,
      "ar": "( فاسم المدبّر ) الذي يشهد كلية كل أمر وجملته التي تبتني عليها جزئياته",
      "en": "The name the Governor witnesses the whole of each affair, upon which its particulars depend.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Account of governance precedes the branching from al-Qayyūm and al-Bāqī.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/50?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-042",
      "page": 50,
      "ar": "فتفنى وتهلك هذه كلّها وتبقى عين وجوده",
      "en": "These [accidents, qualities, determinations and relations] perish, while the very reality of His existence remains.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not construe this as Divine Essence undergoing intrinsic alteration.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/50?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-043",
      "page": 50,
      "ar": "إنه الحقّ المخلوق به كل شيء",
      "en": "It is the Real by which every thing is created.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The relative passive المخلوق به modifies 'every thing': do not misrender as 'the created Real'.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/50?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-044",
      "page": 50,
      "ar": "فإذا وكل العبد أموره إليه يقوم بجميع أموره بحكم الوكالة",
      "en": "When the servant entrusts his affairs to Him, He undertakes all of them on his behalf by delegated care.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Explains how the divine name al-Wakīl branches from al-Qayyūm.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/50?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR005-045",
      "page": 50,
      "ar": "حقيقة الحياة إنما هي صورة القابلية الأولى",
      "en": "The reality of life is the form of the first receptivity.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "This opens the transition to p51 on original love and desire for manifestation; do not confuse essential receptivity with change or deprivation in the Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/50?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,

      "page": 48,
      "ar": "إن اسم الحيّ الذي هو عين الكمال ومنبعه",
      "en": "The name the Living is the very reality and source of perfection.",

      "en": "None of them can claim greatness or self-exaltation; hence the determination of the divine name the Supremely Great arises.",

      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/54?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-020",
      "page": 54,
      "ar": "انتشأ منه ( اسم القابض )",
      "en": "From it arises the name the Withholder.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Paired with al-Bāsiṭ: restraining versus extending what sustains a thing, illustrated by water, breath, desire and resources.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/54?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-021",
      "page": 54,
      "ar": "( واسم المختار ) وإن لم يرد في الشرع لكن فعله وأثره ورد",
      "en": "Although the name the Chooser has not come down in revealed law, its action and effect have.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "This is the author's reported criterion, not an external legal ruling verified here. Distinguish an unattested name-form from an attested divine action.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/54?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-022",
      "page": 55,
      "ar": "وأما القائل",
      "en": "As for the Speaker.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Independent *internal* cross-reference at B2007 p55 alongside p52 and p56 makes p38 printed القابل suspect, pending photographed I1876 and manuscript evidence.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/55?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-023",
      "page": 55,
      "ar": "( اسم الحميد ) بمعنى الحامد . وأمّا بمعنى المحمود ، فهو من فروع اسم الحيّ",
      "en": "The Praiseworthy as One Who praises derives from the Speaker; as the One praised, it branches from the Living.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The same name is affiliated with distinct sources under different active and passive senses: a core example of perspectival derivation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/55?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-024",
      "page": 55,
      "ar": "فهو الشاكر وهو المشكور",
      "en": "He is at once the One Who gives thanks and the One thanked.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The divine name al-Shakūr is explained with the reception and origin of every grace.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/55?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-025",
      "page": 56,
      "ar": "ثم لما كان أصل التكوين مضافا إلى اسم القائل",
      "en": "Since the origin of bringing-into-being is assigned to the name the Speaker.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Third authorial internal cross-reference supporting the p38 القائل conjecture; do not label as external edition witness.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/56?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-026",
      "page": 56,
      "ar": "فإن الخلق في اللغة جاء على ثلاثة معان ، أحدها : التقدير",
      "en": "In the language, creation has three senses, the first being determination of measure.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The following two are combining and cutting. The three lexical senses explain changing name affiliations.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/56?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-027",
      "page": 56,
      "ar": "فهو من توابع اسم القادر والمريد والقائل والعالم والحيّ والجواد والمقسط على السواء",
      "en": "It follows alike upon the Powerful, Willing, Speaker, Knowing, Living, Generous and Equitable.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The explicit enumeration of all seven basic names *includes القائل*, powerfully constraining p38 digital القابل; still not a manuscript variant.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/56?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-028",
      "page": 56,
      "ar": "فالقدرة ظاهرة والقوة باطنها",
      "en": "Power is its outward aspect, while strength is its inward aspect.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī distinguishes qudra at the actual onset of action from quwwa prior to commencement.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/56?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-029",
      "page": 57,
      "ar": "فالخالق يقدّر الصور كلّها ، والباري يصوّر الصور الروحانية والمثالية ، والمصوّر يصور الصور الحسّية",
      "en": "The Creator determines all forms; the Originator fashions spiritual and imaginal forms; the Fashioner forms sensible forms.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish three creative aspects instead of rendering all as generic 'creation'. The text assigns non-sensory forms specifically to al-Bāriʾ.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/57?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-030",
      "page": 57,
      "ar": "أعني على غير مثال خارج عن علمه تعالى",
      "en": "That is, without a model external to His knowledge.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Explicitly qualifies 'without precedent': creative novelty is not outside the timeless Divine knowledge.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/57?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-031",
      "page": 57,
      "ar": "فكانت هذه الأسماء من وجه من توابع العالم ، ومن وجه من توابع القادر",
      "en": "From one perspective these names are subordinate to the Knowing and from another to the Powerful.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "One name can have multiple conceptual affiliations without contradiction.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/57?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-032",
      "page": 58,
      "ar": "ويشبه أن يكون قدمه كناية عن آخر صورة",
      "en": "It seems possible that His 'foot' stands figuratively for the final form.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī introduces this as a tentative figurative reading of the ḥadīth, not a binding literal doctrine or a proven sole interpretation.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/58?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-033",
      "page": 58,
      "ar": "إضافة الأحكام والتأثيرات إلى الأسباب ظاهرة ، وإلى مسبّبها باطنة",
      "en": "Attribution of rulings and effects to causes is outwardly manifest, while attribution to their true Causer remains hidden.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish proximate secondary causes in the world from disclosure of ultimate sovereignty; avoids overreading as denial of causal order.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/58?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-034",
      "page": 58,
      "ar": "وأما الدار الآخرة ، فهي دار كشف وظهور كل شيء على ما هو عليه",
      "en": "The abode of the Hereafter is the abode of disclosure, where everything appears as it truly is.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Explains why al-Malik is specially disclosed as 'Master of the Day of Recompense', although sovereign in both abodes.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/58?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-035",
      "page": 59,
      "ar": "ومن توابع اسم الملك أيضا ( الاسم الحكم )",
      "en": "Among the branches of the King is also the name the Judge.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The Judging name acts through right adjudication and restoring rightful holdings.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/59?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-036",
      "page": 59,
      "ar": "فيتصرّف فيهم تصرف الملاك على ظواهرهم وبواطنهم ، لا تصرف الملوك على ظواهرهم دون بواطنهم",
      "en": "He governs them with ownership over their outward and inward realities, unlike kings who control the outward alone.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Careful contrast between divine ownership of very selves, hearts and spirits versus ordinary political dominion.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/59?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-037",
      "page": 59,
      "ar": "فجميع الصور ملكه والأرواح والقلوب ملكه",
      "en": "All forms belong to Him, and spirits and hearts belong to Him.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Extends ownership to intelligible and spiritual, not merely external jurisdiction.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/59?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-038",
      "page": 60,
      "ar": "ثم يسلمهم إلى ( اسم المعيد ) الذي يعيد الخلق إلى ما ابتدىء منه نزولهم طورا بعد طور",
      "en": "He then hands them over to the name the Restorer, who returns creatures step by step to the source from which their descent began.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Return mirrors descent rank by rank; both concern determinate existence, not spatial travel to an absolute location.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/60?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-039",
      "page": 60,
      "ar": "ومنها إماتة قلبه بالقسوة من حياة الفطرة التي فطر عليها",
      "en": "Among its senses is the death of a person's heart through hardness, extinguishing its primordial life.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "One of several differentiated deaths under al-Mumīt: physical death, death of fitra through hardness, and discipline of the commanding soul.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/60?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR006-040",
      "page": 60,
      "ar": "ومن توابعه أيضا ( الاسم الصبور )",
      "en": "Another of its branches is the name the Forbearing.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Defined below through completion without fatigue and postponing punishment despite the power to prevent opposition.",
      "source_siglum": "B2007",
      "source_url": "https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/60?lang=ar",
      "grade": "modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    }
  ],
  "latest_historical_print_update": {
    "date": "2026-11-09",
    "printed_loci_aggregate": 15,
    "differences_aggregate": 7,
    "agreements_aggregate": 8,
    "edited_lemma_at_p52": "اسم الحكم",
    "verified_manuscript_variants": 0
  }
};

    {

      "page": 61,
      "ar": "وبإظهار آثار هذه الأخلاق فيه والمعاملة معه بها خامسا",
      "en": "Fifth, by showing the effects of those noble traits in the servant and treating him in accordance with them.",
      "disposition": "annotate",

    {

    {

      "en": "The meaning of forgiveness (ghafr) is covering something to preserve it from what would disgrace it.",

      "en": "With it He covers the form of deviation designated as sin.",

      "ar": "وعلى المطيعين خفي توفيقه حين أضاف الأفعال والأعمال والجزاء إليهم لطفا بهم",
      "en": "From the obedient He conceals His hidden enabling grace, attributing deeds and their recompense to them in kindness.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "A paradox of divine agency and human responsibility; preserve causal rather than antinomian framing.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-012",
      "page": 62,
      "ar": "ثم ستر على أوليائه جميع ذلك حتى شاهدوا الكل منه فضلا",
      "en": "He then conceals all these from His friends, so that they witness everything as His favor.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Different modes of concealment for sinners, obedient servants and friends.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-013",
      "page": 62,
      "ar": "بحيث يعفو ، أي يندرس ذلك الأثر والحكم",
      "en": "Thus He pardons: the effect and its judgment are effaced.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "ʿAfw is explained as the erasure of an effect, not simply overlooking blame.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-014",
      "page": 62,
      "ar": "لأن الرأفة ألطف رحمة باطنة منبعثة من الحبّ والعناية",
      "en": "For raʾfa is a subtler inward mercy arising from love and concern.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "A different aspect of mercy than the concealment under ghafūr.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-015",
      "page": 62,
      "ar": "لانتفاء قابلية قبولهم أثر تلك الرحمة الاختصاصية حال الاقتراف أو خفاء تلك القابلية فيهم عندها",
      "en": "Because their receptivity to particular mercy is missing, or hidden, during the offense.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The disjunction أو matters; do not assert that the capability is permanently abolished.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    {
      "id": "CR007-016",
      "page": 63,
      "ar": "التواب هو العوّاد إلى الرحمة",
      "en": "Al-Tawwāb is the One who repeatedly returns to mercy.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Cited as the statement of Abū Madyan; keep attribution separate from Prophetic report.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

      "historical_print_verified": false,

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-017",
      "page": 63,
      "ar": "فإن ما عداهما ممّا يتعلق بهذه النشأة الدنيوية كلّها أمانة وعارية واجب ردّها",
      "en": "All other possessions of this worldly life are a trust or loan required to be returned.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The prior reference is faith and enablement of good works, continuing as eternal gifts.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",
      "source_page_marker": "63",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-Arabic-transcription-derived-from-modern-print; not physical B2007 or independent witness",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-018",
      "page": 63,
      "ar": "فالفتح إبداء خير من مضيق خفائية وإظهار سعة ونور من مضيق ظلمة إجمال",
      "en": "Opening is the disclosure of a good from narrow hiddenness, breadth and light from the constriction of undifferentiated obscurity.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Māniʿ here is not a spatial doorway alone: the governing contrast is metaphysical articulation from latent concentration.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

      "historical_print_verified": false,

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-019",
      "page": 63,
      "ar": "ثم في فتح الصور المثالية من مادة عين الهباء",
      "en": "Next, the opening of imaginal forms from the very matter of primordial dust (habāʾ).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not mistake imaginal for merely subjective fantasy.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-020",
      "page": 63,
      "ar": "ثم في فتح الصور العنصرية من عين مادة الهباء والعنصر الأعظم",
      "en": "Then, the opening of elemental forms from the primordial dust and the supreme element.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "A distinct stage from imaginal forms, preserving the author's hierarchical cosmology.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-021",
      "page": 63,
      "ar": "ثم في فتح مشيمة كل أنثى من الحيوان والإنسان لقبول النطفة بعد ضيقها وارتتاقها",
      "en": "Then, the opening of the generative membrane of each female animal or human to receive the seed after constriction and closure.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "مشيمة literally may designate placenta/afterbirth in later Arabic, but the author's preconception function suggests 'uterine membrane'; do not replace with anatomically modern 'placenta' without historical lexical study.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-022",
      "page": 63,
      "ar": "وأتمّه وأعمّه نفعا وحكما فتح باب تولّد القلب من مضيق مشيمة النفس",
      "en": "Most comprehensive is opening the birth of the heart from the constriction of the soul's enveloping membrane.",

      "critical_note": "Retain repeated مشيمة imagery as a deliberate analogy of a spiritual birth, not an accidental metaphor.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-023",
      "page": 63,
      "ar": "ثم أعلاه وأكمله وأولاه الفتح المبين وهو فتح التجلّي وكشف الأنوار الحقيّة",
      "en": "Highest of all is the manifest opening: divine self-disclosure and unveiling of the lights of the Real.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The series culminates in maʿrifa, not merely intellectual discrimination.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-024",
      "page": 64,
      "ar": "فإمداد الصور الروحانية برزق العلوم والمشاهدات",
      "en": "Spiritual forms are sustained through sciences and acts of witnessing.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The word rizq has a genuine analogical extension beyond physical food.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-025",
      "page": 64,
      "ar": "الذي يعطي كفاف كل موجود بما به قوامه من القوت والقوة بحيث لا ينقص ولا يفضل",
      "en": "The One who gives each being precisely its sufficient nourishment and strength, neither less nor more.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Al-Muqīt is defined by fitting sufficiency (kafāf), not sheer abundance.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    {
      "id": "CR007-026",
      "page": 64,
      "ar": "فكان الغنى والمغني بهذا المعنى من توابع الجواد",
      "en": "Under this sense, self-sufficiency and enrichment are branches of the Generous.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "This classification is conditional upon the sense of possession and dispensing of resources.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-027",
      "page": 64,
      "ar": "وأما بمعنى عدم الاحتياج فهو من أسماء السلبية الذاتية لا الصفاتية",
      "en": "When it means absence of need, it belongs to the negative names of Essence, not the attributive names.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Key cross-category shift: al-Ghanī is not permanently subordinated to al-Jawād regardless of semantic regard.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-028",
      "page": 64,
      "ar": "أما نفعه للقلوب ، فبالتجلّيات والمشاهدات",
      "en": "His benefit to hearts consists in self-disclosures and acts of witnessing.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-029",

      "en": "His benefit to spirits consists in gnoses, sciences and unveilings.",
      "disposition": "retain",

    {
      "id": "CR007-030",
      "page": 64,
      "ar": "فهذه الأسماء كلّها من فروع اسم الجواد",
      "en": "All these names are branches of the Generous.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Closes the specific jūd-branching exposition before al-Muqsiṭ.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-031",
      "page": 65,
      "ar": "حتى إن عين ذلك الاسم أو الحقيقة هو وحدة جمعية تلك الفروع",
      "en": "The very reality of a name or a reality is the comprehensive unity of its branches.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "A genuine whole–part/inclusion principle, not identity of linguistic strings.",

    {
      "id": "CR007-032",
      "page": 65,
      "ar": "بحيث إنه مهما غلب وظهر حكم واحد من هذه الأسماء والحقائق في تلك الوحدة خفي أثر اسم الجواد",
      "en": "Whenever one derivative name predominates in that unity, the effect of the Generous becomes hidden.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Phenomenological dominance changes what appears without eliminating the inclusive source.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-033",
      "page": 65,
      "ar": "وحافظ وحدة جمعيتها إنما هو الاسم المقسط وفرعه الذي هو العدل",
      "en": "What preserves the unity of their inclusiveness is al-Muqsiṭ and its branch al-ʿAdl.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish conservation of a comprehensive unity from equal distribution of quantities.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-034",
      "page": 65,
      "ar": "برعاية حفظ الوسطية فيها بين الوجوب والاستحالة والبرزخية بينهما",
      "en": "By preserving the middle position between necessity and impossibility and the intermediacy between them.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Potentially relevant to contingent existence: mumkin sits between metaphysical impossibility and necessity.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    {
      "id": "CR007-035",
      "page": 65,
      "ar": "فهذا الاسم اسم كلّي سار بجميع معانيه المذكورة في جميع الأسماء",
      "en": "This name [al-Rabb] is universal, pervading all the names in its multiple meanings.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The antecedent is al-Rabb, not al-Muqsiṭ. The text begins a new parent category here.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-036",
      "page": 66,
      "ar": "وهو - أعني ذلك الاسم - مليكه وسيّده والقريب اللازم له وممدّه بالوجود مع الآنات بالخلق الجديد دائما",
      "en": "That name is his sovereign, master, enduring near presence, and supplier of being at successive instants through continual new creation.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Al-khalq al-jadīd is explicit. Do not flatten into the vague idea 'constant divine care'.",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-037",
      "page": 66,
      "ar": "وهي أن الربوبية لها حكمان : حكم عام وحكم خاص",
      "en": "Lordship has two judgments: a general one and a particular one.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This prevents misreading an individual's determining name as a separate lord.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-038",
      "page": 66,
      "ar": "من جهتي الحقيقة القابلة والوجود الظاهر فيها",
      "en": "In two respects: the receptive reality and the existence appearing within it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The name Allāh is universal relative to both intelligible receptive reality and manifested being.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

      "source_page_marker": "66",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-Arabic-transcription-derived-from-modern-print; not physical B2007 or independent witness",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-039",
      "page": 66,
      "ar": "وأما الحكم الخاص الذي للربوبية هو ما ذكرنا أن كل ما تعيّن وجوده أولا من حضرة اسم كان ذلك الاسم ربّه الخاص",
      "en": "Particular lordship is that everything whose existence first becomes determined in a name's presence has that name as its proper Lord.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The rule operates inside general Divine Lordship, not as polytheistic causal independence.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",
      "source_page_marker": "66",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-040",
      "page": 66,
      "ar": "فيكون التجلّي الثاني من حيث ذلك الأثر الخفي الاختصاصي رب ذلك",
      "en": "The second self-disclosure, in respect of that hidden particularizing effect, becomes the Lord of that—",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Sentence crosses B2007 p66–67; preserve the referent completed by الكامل.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-041",
      "page": 67,
      "ar": "ومن قارب الكمّل قابلية واستعدادا وحيطة وكلية وذوقا وشهودا",
      "en": "Those approaching the perfected in receptivity, preparation, inclusiveness, universality, taste and witnessing.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "A graded, not binary, perfection hierarchy.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",
      "source_page_marker": "67",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-Arabic-transcription-derived-from-modern-print; not physical B2007 or independent witness",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-042",
      "page": 67,
      "ar": "أو من أنهر فروع الفروع ، أو من جداول تلك الأنهر ، أو من السواقي",
      "en": "Or from rivers branching from their branches, then their streams and channels.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Water-system analogy is hierarchical determination and return, not literal physical cosmography.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-043",
      "page": 67,
      "ar": "فبحسب الاستعداد يكون تعيّنهم أولا ومرجعهم آخرا",
      "en": "Their first determination and final return accord with their preparedness.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Beginning and return are correlative within the same governing divine name.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

      "historical_print_verified": false,

    {
      "id": "CR007-044",
      "page": 67,
      "ar": "وأما نبيّنا محمد صلى اللّه عليه وسلم فله المنهل الأعلى ، وهو التجلّي الأول الذي هو نوره أولا ، وربّه ثانيا",
      "en": "Our Prophet Muḥammad has the supreme spring: the first self-disclosure, his light first and his Lord in a second regard.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "A technical Akbarī ontological claim, not an identification of a created human person with the Divine Essence.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR007-045",
      "page": 67,
      "ar": "وكلماته إنما هي الأسماء الذاتية المسمّاة بمفاتيح الغيب",
      "en": "Its words are the essential names called the Keys of the Unseen.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "These are not identical with ordinary uttered syllables; see earlier 'names of names' discussion.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    {
      "id": "CR007-046",
      "page": 68,
      "ar": "فهي تنفد وتنتهي عند الرجوع إلى التجلّي الأول",
      "en": "They come to an end on returning to the first self-disclosure.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Relative cessation of derivative determinations in ascent, not exhaustion of divine pre-eternal knowledge.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-047",
      "page": 68,
      "ar": "فإن الوجود الذي هو عين الرحمة التي وسعت كل شيء ، وهو عين الرحمن له جهتان",
      "en": "Existence, identical with all-encompassing mercy and the All-merciful, has two aspects.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The two aspects concern bestowing and being bestowed, not two entities with rival essences.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-048",
      "page": 68,
      "ar": "إحداهما : أنه مفيض على الحقائق الكونية لإظهار الكمالات المتعلقة بها",
      "en": "In the first, it bestows upon cosmic realities, disclosing their perfections.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Agentive consideration of existence as effusion.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",
      "source_page_marker": "68",

      "historical_print_verified": false,

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-049",
      "page": 68,
      "ar": "وأما جهته الثانية : كونه مفاضا مضافا إلى الكائنات في المراتب الكونية",
      "en": "In the second, it is bestowed and predicated of creatures within their cosmic ranks.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "A precise difference between mūfiḍ and mufāḍ central to the user's ontology comparison.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",
      "source_page_marker": "68",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-Arabic-transcription-derived-from-modern-print; not physical B2007 or independent witness",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-050",
      "page": 68,
      "ar": "فلا جرم كلمات اللّه في النزول والتعلّق بالأبدية لا تتناهى",
      "en": "Hence God's words are inexhaustible in descent and their relation to everlastingness.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This and return's termination are not inconsistent because they refer to distinct directions.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3203-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%88%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B3%D9%85%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D9%85%D9%86%D9%87%D8%A7-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B5%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",
      "source_page_marker": "68",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR007-051",
      "page": 68,
      "ar": "فإن حرف الهاء مع الواو يدلّ على الذات غالبا من حيث ظاهر حضرة الهوية",
      "en": "The letter hāʾ with wāw generally indicates Essence in the outward aspect of the Presence of Identity.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Author's letter symbolism must not be presented as a universal rule of Arabic grammar.",

    {

      "ar": "فإن قلت : أن لو حرف امتناع شيء لامتناع غيره",

    {

      "ar": "وكانت المحبّة الأصلية المعنيّة بأحببت وصلة بينهما",
      "en": "The primordial love meant by 'I loved' joins hiddenness and manifestation.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The author's systematic link from the first to the second foundation.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-003",
      "page": 70,
      "ar": "فبهذه المحبة الأصلية واقتضائها تعيّن وبدا وهي سارية فيه كامنة في باطنه",
      "en": "It became determined and appeared through primordial love, which pervades it while remaining concealed within.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Love is not a chronologically later created impulse but an ontological explanatory principle in this system.",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR008-004",
      "page": 70,
      "ar": "فإنه لا أثر للشيء في شيء إلّا بأمر باطن فيه أو منه",
      "en": "Nothing has an effect upon something else except through a reality inwardly present in it or issuing from it.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "This states a specific doctrine of inward causation, not a loose claim that every effect has an unseen cause.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

      "historical_print_verified": false,

    },
    {
      "id": "CR008-005",
      "page": 70,
      "ar": "لم يخل شيء من أثر المحبّة والعشق والطلب والشوق أصلا",
      "en": "Nothing is devoid of some effect of love, attachment, seeking and longing.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Universal extension to cosmic forms is analogical and conditioned by their respective ranks.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
