~~~javascript
    {
      "id": "CR008-006",
      "page": 71,
      "ar": "فظهرت المفاتيح بحكم ذلك السريان من باطن كل اسم وصفة وحقيقة إلهيّة",
      "en": "The Keys of the Unseen become manifest from the inward aspect of every divine name, attribute and reality.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Causes of activity appear through names, while recipient preparedness appears in cosmic realities.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3204-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AB%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%83%D8%A7%D9%85-%D9%85%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B1%D9%88%D8%A7%D8%AD-%D9%88%D8%B9%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%84%D9%83%D9%88%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR008-007",
      "page": 71,
      "ar": "فكل واحد من الحقائق يرجع بحكم هذا الطلب والشوق والسؤال الحالي والاستعدادي إلى أصله",
      "en": "Each reality returns, through desire and the petition of state and preparedness, to its own origin.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "A claim about existential dependence through preparedness, not necessarily a verbal supplication.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR008-008",
      "page": 71,
      "ar": "إلى الأصول السبعة المتوقّف عليها الحكم والأمر الإيجادي",
      "en": "Ultimately to the seven principles upon which the creative judgment and command depend.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The seven principals remain the indispensable intelligible relations for creative disclosure.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR008-009",
      "page": 71,
      "ar": "وهذه ثاني دورة معنوية للمحبّة الأصلية",
      "en": "This is the second intelligible circuit of primordial love.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The word دورة معنوية marks a non-temporal cycle. Avoid literal cosmic chronology.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3204-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AB%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%83%D8%A7%D9%85-%D9%85%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B1%D9%88%D8%A7%D8%AD-%D9%88%D8%B9%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%84%D9%83%D9%88%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    {
      "id": "CR008-010",
      "page": 71,
      "ar": "فعادت مأذونة مسارعة إلى النزول سارية في المفاتيح",
      "en": "Having received permission, it returns and hastens downward through the Keys.",
      "disposition": "retain",

    {
      "id": "CR008-019",
      "page": 73,
      "ar": "فالماء مثل الحقيقة القابلة والجدار الصقيل مثل المرتبة",
      "en": "The water signifies the receptive reality; the polished wall signifies the rank.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Analogy of reflected light must preserve both the receiving reality and its locus.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {
      "id": "CR008-020",
      "page": 73,
      "ar": "فهذه تمثيل مطابق من بعض الوجوه لا كلّها",
      "en": "This analogy holds in some respects, not in all.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-021",
      "page": 73,
      "ar": "ثم مد ظل تعيّنات الوجود المفاض على الكائنات والمضاف إليها في مرتبة الأرواح والمثال والحسّ",
      "en": "He then stretches the shadow of determinations of bestowed existence ascribed to creatures in spiritual, imaginal and sensible ranks.",

      "critical_note": "The successive 'shadows' are ontological dependence metaphors, not local displacement.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-022",
      "page": 73,
      "ar": "فكان هذا المدّ منه على سبيل الإرادة والاختيار",
      "en": "This extension takes place through will and choice.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Author explicitly rejects necessary emanation ('by essence') in favor of chosen disclosure.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-023",
      "page": 73,
      "ar": "وذلك القبض مثل رجوع الوجود كل آن إلى أصله",
      "en": "That withdrawal resembles existence returning to its source at every instant.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Core passage for a rigorous comparison with theories of continuous creation.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-024",
      "page": 73,
      "ar": "لكونه عرضا على الحقيقة وقيام بدل مثله مكانه في الخلق الجديد",
      "en": "Because it is truly an accident, and its like stands in its place in renewed creation.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "The referent of عرضا may be the bestowed existence rather than absolute Being; investigate context and other print/MS witnesses before equating with Ashʿarite occasionalism.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-025",
      "page": 73,
      "ar": "على نحو رجوع الغذاء والدهن بالتحليل من البدن والسراج إلى ما بدا منه من الأركان",
      "en": "Like food and oil returning by dissolution from body and lamp to the elements from which they arose.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "An analogy of ongoing replacement with continuity at the manifest level, not a proof of substantial motion.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3204-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AB%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%83%D8%A7%D9%85-%D9%85%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B1%D9%88%D8%A7%D8%AD-%D9%88%D8%B9%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D9%84%D9%83%D9%88%D8%AA-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",

    {
      "id": "CR008-026",
      "page": 74,
      "ar": "ظهر مفصّلا في صنفين",
      "en": "It appears differentiated into two categories.",
      "disposition": "retain",

    {

      "en": "First, it is a composite configuration from the rays of bestowed and attributed light.",

      "en": "One aspect involves direct relation, under which it is named a Spirit ascribed to the divine Presence.",

      "en": "The dimensions of the world derived from it are six: above, below, right, left, before and behind.",

      "id": "CR008-038",

    {
      "id": "CR008-039",
      "page": 76,
      "ar": "ففي إيجاد الأرواح كان القول ركنا ، والقدرة تتمّة له وشرطا وفي الأجسام بالعكس",
      "en": "For the origination of spirits, speech is a pillar and power its completion and condition; for bodies the reverse holds.",

      "id": "CR008-040",

      "ar": "فإن إفاضة الوجود بالاختيار لا يكون إلّا عن جود بوجود",
      "en": "Bestowing existence by choice occurs only through generosity with existence.",
      "disposition": "retain",

    {
      "id": "CR008-041",
      "page": 76,
      "ar": "وأما الإقساط ، فهو شرط في ذلك ، ولكن بسراية المفاتيح في القوابل",
      "en": "Equity is likewise a condition, but through the Keys' pervasion of receptive realities.",

      "critical_note": "Differentiates recipient/acceptance condition from active command.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-042",

      "ar": "فكان إسرافيل مظهرا لركن الحياة الكلّية الجملية الأصلية",
      "en": "Thus Isrāfīl was a manifestation of universal primordial life.",
      "disposition": "retain",

    {

    {
      "id": "CR008-048",

      "en": "The second subgroup is made dependent on their manifestations.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-049",

      "en": "The spirit precedes the determination of the person's elemental temperament.",
      "disposition": "annotate",

    {
      "id": "CR008-050",
      "page": 78,
      "ar": "فإن كل واحد من هؤلاء له قوتان ، ولا بدّ يطيرون بهما",
      "en": "Each messenger has two powers by which he necessarily flies.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Metaphysical exegesis of wings; the literal hadith/narrative tradition is a distinct evidentiary layer.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-051",
      "page": 78,
      "ar": "إحداهما : قوّة علمية آخذه من موجده تعالى ، والثانية : قوة عملية عاملة بموجب ذلك العلم",
      "en": "One is a knowing power receiving from the Creator; the second a practical power acting according to knowledge.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Knowledge and praxis are the two fundamental wings.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-052",
      "page": 78,
      "ar": "وهو تعليم غيره ما علّمه",
      "en": "A third wing consists in teaching another what one has learned.",

      "critical_note": "The pedagogical extension is an additional power beyond knowing and doing.",

    {

      "page": 78,
      "ar": "هو العمل لغيره بإذن ربّه وأمره",
      "en": "A fourth wing is acting for another by the Lord's permission and command.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Intercession and service extend wing symbolism beyond the practitioner's own actions.",

    {
      "id": "CR008-054",
      "page": 78,
      "ar": "فغير محصورة ، وما ورد في بعض الأخبار أن النبيّ صلى اللّه عليه وسلم رأى جبرائيل عليه السلام وله ستمائة جناح",
      "en": "Their particular powers are innumerable; reports describe the Prophet seeing Gabriel with six hundred wings.",
~~~

~~~javascript
    {

    {
      "id": "CR009-003",
      "page": 79,
      "ar": "فجميع ذلك أضحى تفصيلا لهذا الإجمال ، ولكن تفصيلا حقيقيّا لا نسبيّا",

    {
      "id": "CR009-011",

      "ar": "كان اسم الطبيعة نتيجة ذلك الامتزاج ، فكانت الطبيعة برزخا جامعا بين الأركان المذكورة",
      "en": "The term Nature results from that blending; Nature is an isthmus encompassing the mentioned principles.",

    {

    {
      "id": "CR009-016",

      "en": "The Throne's form is divided into twelve conceived, not sensed, divisions.",

    {
      "id": "CR009-017",
      "page": 83,
      "ar": "فكان هذا العرش مستوى الاسم الرحمن على جميع معاني الاستواء",
      "en": "Thus this Throne is the locus of the name the All-merciful under all the senses of istiwāʾ.",
      "disposition": "annotate",

    {
      "id": "CR009-027",
      "page": 86,

    {
      "id": "CR009-031",
      "page": 87,
      "ar": "فكانت تلك الحركة والقوة الشوقية أوجبت محضة فيها قوية مقوية",
      "en": "That yearning motion and force produced in it an intensified potency.",

    {

    {
      "id": "CR009-060",
      "page": 97,
      "ar": "ومن أعظم شروط الخلافة العلم بجميع المراتب وبأهليها وبحقوقهم وأحكامهم",

    {

    {

    {
      "id": "CR009-064",

      "ar": "فلهذا المعنى أسند فعل النفخ إلى نفسه بصيغة الإفراد لا بصيغة الجمع",

    {
      "id": "CR009-065",
      "page": 98,
      "ar": "فإن علم كنه الذات ممتنع ، وعلم الأسماء كلّها ممكن",
      "en": "Knowledge of the Essence as such is impossible, while knowledge of all the names is possible.",

    {
      "id": "CR009-066",
      "page": 99,
      "ar": "والألفاظ المركّبة من الحروف الدالّة على تلك التعيّنات الوجودية هي أسماء تلك الأسماء",
      "en": "Expressions composed of letters that signify those existential determinations are names of the names.",
      "disposition": "retain",

    {
      "id": "CR009-067",
      "page": 99,
      "ar": "فكأنه تعالى وتقدس عرّف وعلّم آدم حقيقة ذاته - أي ذات آدم",
      "en": "It is as though He made Adam know and taught him the reality of his own self—that is, Adam's own self.",

    {
      "id": "CR009-068",
      "page": 99,
      "ar": "فعرف بذلك التعليم والتعريف نفسه ، وبتلك المعرفة ربّه",
      "en": "Through that instruction he knew himself, and through knowing himself he knew his Lord.",

    {
      "id": "CR009-069",
      "page": 100,
      "ar": "فلمّا بان عجزهم وتنصّلوا عما قالوا وما فعلوا عاد إلى تكميلهم بواسطة أصلهم وكلّهم",
      "en": "When their inability became manifest, He resumed their perfection through their source and encompassing whole.",

    {
      "id": "CR009-070",
      "page": 100,

    {
      "id": "CR009-071",
      "page": 100,
      "ar": "حيث كانت الملائكة محصورين بحكم عالمهم ونشأتهم لم يهتدوا إلى معرفة ما كان خارجا عنها",
      "en": "The angels are confined to the rules of their sphere and creation, and cannot know what lies beyond it.",

    {
      "id": "CR009-072",
      "page": 101,
      "ar": "فحجب بوهمه وأبعد بحجّته ولجاجه واعتراضه في مقابلة الأمر المطاع",
      "en": "He was veiled by his imagination and distanced by his argument, obstinacy and opposition to the command.",

    {
      "id": "CR009-073",
      "page": 101,
      "ar": "فإن أحدا لا يظهر شيئا إلّا بما فيه من ذلك بالفعل أو بالقوّة",
      "en": "No one manifests anything unless it already lies within him actually or potentially.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Principle of manifestation invoked in Farghānī's reconstruction of the Iblīs episode.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3213-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AB%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AB-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%B9%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AB%D8%A7%D9%84-%D9%88%D9%85%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%AC%D8%B3%D8%A7%D9%85-%D8%A5%D9%84%D9%89-%D8%AA%D9%83%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%B5%D9%88%D8%B1%D8%A9-%D8%A2%D8%AF%D9%85-%D8%B9%D9%84%D9%8A%D9%87-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%A7%D9%85-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",
      "source_page_marker": "101",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-Arabic-transcription-derived-from-modern-print; not physical B2007 or independent witness",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR009-074",
      "page": 101,
      "ar": "وهذا دليل واضح على أن الملائكة والأرواح والعقول لهم الترقّي والزيادة على خلاف ما زعمت الفلاسفة",
      "en": "This plainly proves, in Farghānī's view, that angels, spirits and intellects can progress and increase—contrary to what the philosophers claimed.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Explicit anti-philosophers conclusion with an ontological/pedagogical argument. Avoid attributing one unified denial of progress to every philosopher without independent historical comparison.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3213-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%B5%D9%84-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AB%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AB-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%B1-%D8%AA%D8%B9%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%B9%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AB%D8%A7%D9%84-%D9%88%D9%85%D8%B1%D8%AA%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D8%AC%D8%B3%D8%A7%D9%85-%D8%A5%D9%84%D9%89-%D8%AA%D9%83%D9%88%D9%86-%D8%B5%D9%88%D8%B1%D8%A9-%D8%A2%D8%AF%D9%85-%D8%B9%D9%84%D9%8A%D9%87-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%A7%D9%85-%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A8-%D9%85%D9%86%D8%AA%D9%87%D9%89-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%AF%D8%A7%D8%B1%D9%83-%D9%81%D9%8A-%D8%B4%D8%B1%D8%AD-%D8%AA%D8%A7%D8%A6%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B3%D9%84%D8%B7%D8%A7%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B9%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%86-%D8%A7%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%B6-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%B2%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A3%D9%88%D9%84-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%B4%D9%8A%D8%AE-%D9%85%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A8%D9%86-%D8%A3%D8%AD%D9%85%D8%AF-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%81%D8%B1%D8%BA%D8%A7%D9%86%D9%8A",
~~~

<!-- BEGIN GNAMMA DATA data/critical-pass-010-p102-125.js SHA 7f38ffc7bc457c8d306c388ee4f4f552232d4961 -->
~~~javascript
window.FARGHANI_CRITICAL_PASS_010={
  "schema": "gnamma-farghani-critical-pass-clause-v2",
  "date": "2026-11-09",
  "stage": "XIV-A",
  "scope": "Foundation IV page labels 102–125; bounded selected authorial Arabic–English checks only",
  "proofing_stage": "Derivative forum retranscription read against working English and context; no printed B2007 photograph, no I1876 or manuscript checked for new loci",
  "records": [
    {
      "id": "CR010-001",
      "page": 102,
      "ar": "أول ما تعين من الغيب الحقيقي إنما كانت الوحدة الحقيقية الذاتية",
      "en": "The first determination from the true Unseen was essential, real unity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This opens Foundation IV by restating its ontological starting point.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "102",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-002",
      "page": 102,
      "ar": "نسبة الأحدية المسقطة للاعتبارات ، ونسبة الواحدية المثبتة جميعها إليها على السّواء",
      "en": "The aspect of unicity that negates considerations and the aspect of oneness that affirms them all are equally related to it.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Aḥadiyya and wāḥidiyya are paired, not two successive temporal stages.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "102",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-003",
      "page": 103,
      "ar": "وأما مقتضى الكمال الذاتي آخرا ، فكمال استجلاء الذات الأقدس الواحد الأحد",
      "en": "The final exigency of essential perfection is the most complete self-vision of the Most Holy Essence.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish jalaʾ from istijlaʾ as two aspects of disclosure.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "103",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-004",
      "page": 103,
      "ar": "وذلك المظهر الحقيقي صوري ، وهو عين المزاج المطهر الأعدل المحمدي ، ومعنوي وهو قلبه التقيّ النقيّ",
      "en": "That true locus is corporeally the purified, most balanced Muhammadan temperament and inwardly his pure, Godwary heart.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The author distinguishes formal bodily and inner-heart aspects of the same locus.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "103",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-005",
      "page": 104,
      "ar": "وهذه كلّها صور اعتبارات الواحدية صورا معنويّة ظاهرة بالنسبة إلى الحقّ من كونها نسبة",
      "en": "All of these are intelligible forms of the considerations of oneness, apparent relative to the Real insofar as they are relations.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Relational form versus independent created substance is a critical distinction.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "104",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-006",
      "page": 104,
      "ar": "فصار كل روح وروحانية كل شيء مظهر الاعتبار من الواحدية",
      "en": "Every spirit and spiritual reality of a thing becomes a locus of one of the considerations of oneness.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Continuation through spiritual, imaginal and sensible ranks.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "104",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-007",
      "page": 105,
      "ar": "فلم يوجد شيء غالبا فيها بغير سبب وعلّة ظاهرة",
      "en": "Ordinarily nothing in this realm comes to be without a manifest cause.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Preserve غالبا: the rule expressly allows exceptions rather than universal physical necessity.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "105",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-008",
      "page": 105,
      "ar": "ثم ابتدأ لكل واحد من مظاهر هذه الأئمّة السبعة الأسمائية والصفاتية",
      "en": "Then for every manifestation of these seven leading names and attributes a further cycle began.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The authorial sentence continues at length to specify the cycle and its purpose; this excerpt deliberately stops at the uninterrupted opening clause.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "105",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-009",
      "page": 106,
      "ar": "فاقتضى التجلّي الثاني من حيث الاسم القائل",
      "en": "The second self-disclosure, under the aspect of the divine name the Speaker, accordingly required [the preparation of Adam's clay].",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The bounded Arabic span ends before the Adamic clay phrase later in the same sentence. The name al-Qāʾil here is *internal* corroboration for the p38 crux, not an independent manuscript variant.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "106",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-010",
      "page": 106,
      "ar": "فكانت صورة آدم عليه السلام الجامعة بين جميع الكمالات أصلا ومنشأ لجميع الصور الإنسانية",
      "en": "Adam's form, encompassing all perfections, became the origin and source of all human forms.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Author's model of collective Adamic humanity; distinguish from empirical genealogy.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "106",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-011",
      "page": 107,
      "ar": "يسمّى ذلك الميزان شريعة",
      "en": "That governing measure is called revealed law (sharīʿa).",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Sharīʿa enters the ontological scheme as the balance governing actions, not merely a legal code.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "107",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-012",
      "page": 107,
      "ar": "فذلك الميزان هو الكتاب العزيز المنزّل على كل خليفة كامل",
      "en": "That measure is the exalted book revealed to each perfected vicegerent.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The claim concerns the author's special class of complete prophetic vicegerents, not every saint.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "107",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-013",
      "page": 108,
      "ar": "فإذا كل كامل خليفة ولا ينعكس",
      "en": "Every perfected one is a vicegerent, but the converse does not hold.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The converse denial is explicit and essential to differentiating perfection from vicegerency.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "108",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-014",
      "page": 108,
      "ar": "وأن الكمّل قليلون والخلفاء كثيرون",
      "en": "The fully perfected are few, while the vicegerents are numerous.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Terminological distinction, not a count of historical caliphs.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "108",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-015",
      "page": 109,
      "ar": "فبطلت الكبائس والنسىء وعاد حكم اقتضاء الزمان والدور إلى حكم الوحدة والاعتدال",
      "en": "The intercalations and postponement of months ceased, and the rule of the cycle returned to unity and balance.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "A speculative cosmic interpretation of the calendrical proclamation; distinguish historical/legal interpretation.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "109",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-016",
      "page": 109,
      "ar": "وتوجّه جميع ما ذكرنا إلى تعيين المزاج الأعدل المحمّدي المذكور",
      "en": "All the preceding determinations converge on the formation of the most balanced Muhammadan temperament.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Within Farghānī's metaphysical model; no external medical or astral verification.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "109",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-017",
      "page": 110,
      "ar": "ومتضمّن أيضا بيان دقائق علم الطريقة المتعلّق بها تكميل الهيئة النفسانية والروحانية",
      "en": "The Qurʾān also explains the subtleties of the path by which psychic and spiritual constitution is perfected.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Pairs with the law governing actions and the truth concerning knowledge of divine names.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "110",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-018",
      "page": 110,
      "ar": "فكان الظاهر والنازل من الغيب إلى الشهادة ؛ كتابين أحدهما كتاب فعلي والثاني كتاب قولي",
      "en": "What manifests from the Unseen into the witnessed world takes two forms of book: a book of acts and a book of speech.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Keep a strict distinction between cosmic manifestation and verbally revealed scripture.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "110",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-019",
      "page": 111,
      "ar": "كانت بمنزلة سورة",
      "en": "It would be analogous to a sūra.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Cosmic book metaphor develops from letter, name, verb, word, verse, sūra to comprehensive book; not literal textual inscription in matter.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "111",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-020",
      "page": 111,
      "ar": "وأما إذا أفاد ذلك الاجتماع المحيط أحدية جمع مضافة إلى حقيقة الحقائق",
      "en": "When this all-encompassing conjunction expresses unicity of gathering related to the Reality of Realities.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The higher universal form is analogized to the Qurʾān, completing the cosmic-book analogy.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "111",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-021",
      "page": 112,
      "ar": "فتلك النقطة الاعتدالية هي نقطة الولاية لقربها من أحدية الغيب المطلق",
      "en": "That point of equilibrium is the point of sainthood, because of its nearness to the unicity of the absolute Unseen.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Wilāya arises as spiritual centrality, not merely office or institutional authority.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "112",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-022",
      "page": 112,
      "ar": "فكان في هذا معنى القرب",
      "en": "In this lies the meaning of nearness.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Root derivation from walāʾ and tawālī is an authorial etymological argument.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "112",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-023",
      "page": 113,
      "ar": "فالنقطة الوسطية الاعتدالية الوحدانية ... إنما هي حقيقة النبوّة",
      "en": "The unifying middle point of balance corresponds to the reality of prophethood.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "A related but distinct centre from the wilāya-centre of the preceding page.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "113",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-024",
      "page": 113,
      "ar": "والنبوّة إن اعتبرت مهموزة ، فهي من الإنباء الإخبار",
      "en": "Prophethood, when the word is pronounced with hamza, derives from informing and giving news.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "One of two lexical derivations, the other from elevation; avoid falsely asserting a sole lexical origin.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "113",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-025",
      "page": 114,
      "ar": "فالنبيّ إذن صاحب إذن إلهي",
      "en": "Thus a prophet is one who possesses divine authorization.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "In the author's functional distinction between prophet and messenger.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "114",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-026",
      "page": 114,
      "ar": "والرسول صاحب أمر ، أي مأمور بالدعوة والتبليغ",
      "en": "The messenger possesses a command: he is ordered to summon and deliver the message.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "A particular doctrinal criterion in Farghānī, not necessarily a consensus definition in kalām.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "114",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-027",
      "page": 115,
      "ar": "غير أن آدم عليه السلام لما كان صاحب هبوط ونزول",
      "en": "But Adam—peace be upon him—was the one of descent and coming down.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Contrasted with Muḥammadan return/ascent, not a moral deficiency in Adam.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "115",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-028",
      "page": 115,
      "ar": "فلا جرم من لدنّ آدم إلى محمد صلى اللّه عليه وسلم كانت النبوّة ظاهرة ، والولاية مخفيّة مندرجة فيها",
      "en": "Thus from Adam to Muḥammad prophethood was outwardly manifest and sainthood hidden within it.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Descent-era aspect of the author's prophetic history, not a rejection of sainthood among earlier prophets.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "115",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-029",
      "page": 116,
      "ar": "فكان اجتماع حقيقة الولاية مع حقيقة النبوّة فيه على السواء",
      "en": "In him the reality of sainthood and prophethood met in equal balance.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Balanced aspects in the Muhammadan reality.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "116",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-030",
      "page": 116,
      "ar": "ولمّا كان صلى اللّه عليه وسلم صاحب عود ورجوع وعروج ؛ كما أن آدم كان صاحب هبوط ونزول",
      "en": "Since he was the one of return and ascent, as Adam had been the one of descent.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The opposite poles are typological, not competing actual persons.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "116",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-031",
      "page": 117,
      "ar": "فلا جرم ختم به النبوّة ، فلا نبيّ بعده أصلا",
      "en": "Hence prophethood was sealed by him: absolutely no prophet follows him.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Author explicitly affirms finality of prophethood.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "117",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-032",
      "page": 117,
      "ar": "كلّهم كانوا أولياء وأقطابا مندرجا حكم النبوّة في ولايتهم",
      "en": "All were saints and poles with the judgment of prophethood contained within their sainthood.",
      "disposition": "crux",
      "critical_note": "This distinguishes spiritual inheritance from any new prophetic commission; careful theological note required.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "117",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-033",
      "page": 118,
      "ar": "كان القرآن جامعا جميع الطرق والأحكام",
      "en": "The Qurʾān comprehends all paths and determinations.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī's theology of comprehensive revelation, not a claim every physical fact is directly stated.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "118",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-034",
      "page": 118,
      "ar": "يفهم ويستنبط ويستخرج من عباراته وإشاراته ودلالاته ومفهوماته",
      "en": "One understands, derives and extracts from its verbal expressions, allusions, indications and conceived meanings.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The four exegetical channels are not identical; important for the planned tafsīr linkage.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "118",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-035",
      "page": 119,
      "ar": "محتاج في كل آن إلى مدد وجودي يرجح جانب بقاء ذلك الشخص على فنائه",
      "en": "At every instant a person needs existential assistance that gives preponderance to remaining over passing away.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Explicit notion of continued actualization, distinct from metaphysical dependence understood only at origin.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "119",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-036",
      "page": 119,
      "ar": "ووصول هذا المدد دائما مع الآنات عبارة عن الخلق الجديد",
      "en": "The continuous arrival of this assistance with the instants is what is meant by renewed creation.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "This provides an especially direct definition of khalq jadīd, parallel to p66 and p73.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "119",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-037",
      "page": 120,
      "ar": "موجب إعراضه وغفلته عن موجده الحق تعالى",
      "en": "It brings about his turning away and heedlessness of the Real who originates him.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The account links impediments in formative descent to spiritual veiling.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "120",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-038",
      "page": 120,
      "ar": "والتسويلات والتسويفات النفسانية والشيطانية",
      "en": "And the soul's and devil's deceptive suggestions and procrastinations.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Lexically distinguish taswīl (persuasive embellishment of vice) from taswīf (putting off action).",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "120",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-039",
      "page": 121,
      "ar": "فالشريعة هي المعينة لبعض تلك الأقوال والأفعال والحركات والسكنات",
      "en": "The Law specifies speech, actions, movements and stillness conducive to removal of veils.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Sharīʿa is the universal regimen; individualized spiritual treatment is separately supplied by experienced guides.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "121",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-040",
      "page": 121,
      "ar": "وعلماء الطريقة والحقيقة الذين هم كبار المشايخ وأطباء تلك العلل والأمراض المعنوية",
      "en": "Those learned in the path and the truth—the great masters—are physicians of spiritual maladies.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The comparison is explicitly therapeutic and analogical.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "121",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-041",
      "page": 122,
      "ar": "فعلى هذا يكون أهم المهمات للسالك الطالب ... حصول شيخ مرشد",
      "en": "Thus the foremost concern for the seeking traveller is obtaining a guiding master.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī's own normative programme, not a neutral universal condition of religion.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "122",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-042",
      "page": 122,
      "ar": "ومظهرا عرفانا كسبيّا ظنّه كشفيّا شهوديّا",
      "en": "And displaying acquired knowledge that he mistakes for unveiled, witnessed gnosis.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Central epistemic warning: acquired/scholarly knowledge may be misidentified as direct spiritual realization.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "122",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-043",
      "page": 123,
      "ar": "ولظاهر هذه البرزخية حكم إجمال وحكم تفصيل",
      "en": "The outer aspect of this intermediacy has a comprehensive judgment and a differentiated judgment.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Maintains barzakh's structural polarity.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "123",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-044",
      "page": 123,
      "ar": "ومزاج سائر الكمل والخلفاء وأولي العزم من الرسل والأقطاب من الأولياء المحمديين هو صورة البرزخية الثانية",
      "en": "The temperament of the other perfected vicegerents and resolute messengers, and Muhammadan poles, images the second intermediacy.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Farghānī's spiritual anthropological hierarchy; a technical rather than empirical temperament claim.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "123",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-045",
      "page": 124,
      "ar": "ففي الرتبة الأولى الكونية تلبس بلباس مظهر روحاني",
      "en": "In the first cosmic rank it wears the form of a spiritual manifestation.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Three ranks: spiritual, imaginal and sensible.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "124",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-046",
      "page": 124,
      "ar": "وهو الذي قامت به الفطرة الأصلية التي فطر الخلق عليها",
      "en": "It is the basis of the primordial disposition upon which creatures are fashioned.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Fitra is associated with the unitary heart in all forms, not exclusively adults.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "124",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-047",
      "page": 125,
      "ar": "حتى غفلت عن أصل فطرتيها المذكورتين",
      "en": "Until the soul forgot the origins of its two primordial dispositions.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The two fitras here are corporeal and spiritual, not two religious communities.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "125",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR010-048",
      "page": 125,
      "ar": "انحرفت أخلاقها وأوصافها بالميل إمّا إلى جانب إفراط أو طرف تفريط",
      "en": "It inclines either to the extreme of excess or to the opposite of deficiency.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "A virtue-ethical balance model underlies spiritual pathology.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "125",
~~~

~~~javascript
      "page": 126,
      "ar": "بموجب جذبة من جذبات الحقّ توازي عمل الثقلين",
      "en": "Through an attraction of the Real equalling the work of the two orders of beings.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Reported spiritual dictum in authorial discourse; hadith/source grading separate. Attraction is contrasted with acquired discipline.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",

      "id": "CR011-002",
      "page": 126,
      "ar": "وهذا الأخذ في السير متعلّق بمقام الإسلام",
      "en": "Commencing the journey belongs to the station of Islam.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "First level of the sequence Islam, faith, and excellence; the second is estrangement and the third contemplative disclosure.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "126",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-003",
      "page": 127,
      "ar": "أما المهم الأول : الذي هو الأخذ في السير ، فمنقسم على ثلاثة أقسام",
      "en": "The first task, setting out on the journey, is divided into three sections.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Beginnings, Doors, and Transactions are differentiated rather than collapsed into a single method.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "127",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-004",
      "page": 127,
      "ar": "مقام التوبة : وهي الرجوع من المخالفة إلى الموافقة",
      "en": "The station of repentance is the return from transgression to conformity.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Repentance defined relationally through return, before later intensifications.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "127",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-005",
      "page": 128,
      "ar": "وثالثة : الرياضة ، التي هي إزالة الشماس",
      "en": "Third is discipline: the removal of recalcitrance.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Shamās is a deliberately equestrian lexical image of an ungovernable temperament; it is not passive physical exercise.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "128",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-006",
      "page": 128,
      "ar": "فأولها وأهمّها : الزهد",
      "en": "The first and most important is renunciation (zuhd).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The first of the three Doors; scrupulousness and sorrow follow.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "128",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-007",
      "page": 129,
      "ar": "وما بسبب هو التوكّل ، وبلا سبب هو الثقة",
      "en": "Where means are involved, it is reliance; where there are no means, confidence.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "An unusually precise distinction within entrusting matters, not a denial of ordinary causal means.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "129",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-008",
      "page": 129,
      "ar": "وهذا هو التجلّي الفعلي",
      "en": "This is self-disclosure through action.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Al-Tajallī al-fiʿlī follows the path's moral reordering, distinct from disclosures through attributes.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "129",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-009",
      "page": 130,
      "ar": "فنشأة النفس حسّية شهادية وحكمها مختصّ بمرتبة الإسلام",
      "en": "The constitution of the soul is sensible and witnessed, and its rule pertains to Islam.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Keep the three aspects of the human constitution separate, corresponding to Islam, faith and excellence.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "130",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-010",
      "page": 130,
      "ar": "ونشأة الروح غيبية إضافية كونية ، وحكمها مختصّ بباطن الإيمان وسرّه",
      "en": "The spirit's constitution belongs to a relational cosmic unseen, and its rule to the inward reality of faith.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The inner spiritual dimension is still a created and relational unseen, not Absolute Unseen.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "130",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-011",
      "page": 131,
      "ar": "فأعمّ الأخلاق حكما وأشملها أثرا إنما هو الصبر",
      "en": "Patience has the widest rule and the broadest effect among character traits.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Patience extends through all stations and is not merely endurance of adversity.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "131",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-012",
      "page": 131,
      "ar": "والركن الثالث : الرضاء",
      "en": "The third pillar is contentment (riḍā).",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The author explicitly distinguishes contentment with God's wisdom from approval of acts violating the Law.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "131",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-013",
      "page": 132,
      "ar": "فالأول هو القصد الصحيح في التوجه عن بصيرة وطمأنينة",
      "en": "The first is right intention in turning with discernment and assurance.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "One of the fundamental pillars after character discipline.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "132",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-014",
      "page": 132,
      "ar": "وهو اليقين من حيث رتبته الثانية ، التي هي عين اليقين",
      "en": "This is certainty in its second rank, certainty of direct vision (ʿayn al-yaqīn).",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Distinguish ʿilm al-yaqīn, ʿayn al-yaqīn and ḥaqq al-yaqīn in the progression.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "132",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-015",
      "page": 133,
      "ar": "فيكون متحقّقا بحقيقة الفقر",
      "en": "He becomes realized in the reality of poverty (faqr).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Poverty here names a metaphysical and spiritual dependence, not merely lack of money.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "133",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-016",
      "page": 133,
      "ar": "فحنّت الروح الروحانية بأحكامها إلى النفس الإنسانية",
      "en": "The spiritual spirit, with its determining characteristics, yearns toward the human soul.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "The subsequent conjugal imagery expresses the reunion and harmonization of two dimensions of the person.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "133",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-017",
      "page": 134,
      "ar": "فتولد بحكم اجتماعهما عن مشيمة جمعية النفس ولد قلب حقيقي",
      "en": "Their union gives birth, from the soul's encompassing membrane, to a true heart.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "Spiritual birth imagery: preserve relation to p63 مشيمة, not literal anatomical claim.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "134",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-018",
      "page": 134,
      "ar": "داخلا في مبدأ الحضرات الحقية المسمّى بمقام الإحسان",
      "en": "Entering the beginning of the presences of the Real, called the station of excellence (iḥsān).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The transition is from the stations of the spirit into contemplative disclosure.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "134",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-019",
      "page": 135,
      "ar": "فأوّلها : الغيرة المقتضية إزالة الغيرية",
      "en": "First is protective jealousy, which requires the removal of otherness.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Ghayra/ghayriyya forms a technical paronomasia and should be retained in notes.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "135",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-020",
      "page": 135,
      "ar": "ثم الشوق الذي هو أثر الغيرة",
      "en": "Then longing, which is an effect of protective jealousy.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The affective progression has ordered degrees and is not merely a generic description of mystical feelings.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "135",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-021",
      "page": 136,
      "ar": "الزمان المسمّى باصطلاح القوم الوقت",
      "en": "Time, termed the moment (waqt) in the usage of the people of the path.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Waqt here marks a spiritual present, rather than an arbitrary clock interval.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "136",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-022",
      "page": 136,
      "ar": "فكان اللّحظ والوقت والصفاء من مقاماته",
      "en": "Attention, the moment, and clarity are among its stations.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Do not misread the sequence as self-standing created substances.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "136",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-023",
      "page": 137,
      "ar": "فتلك النقطة هي مقام التمكين",
      "en": "That point is the station of stabilization (tamkīn).",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Stabilization is the steadiness of the traveler in relation to changing disclosures.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "137",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-024",
      "page": 137,
      "ar": "والرتبة الثالثة : مرتبة الجمع والبرزخية بينهما",
      "en": "The third rank is that of gathering and the intermediacy between them.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Third rank mediates between outward and inward, not simply another alternating emotional state.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "137",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-025",
      "page": 138,
      "ar": "أحدهما : من جهة كونه مفيضا ، والآخر من جهة كونه مفاضا",
      "en": "One aspect is its being the giver of existence, the other its being bestowed.",
      "disposition": "revise",
      "critical_note": "The central mūfiḍ/mufāḍ ontological polarity recurs in the spiritual psychology.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",
      "source_url": "https://alshrefalm7sy.ahlamontada.com/t3214",
      "source_page_marker": "138",
      "source_grade": "secondary-online-retranscription-of-modern-edited-Arabic; selected authorial span; no original print or manuscript photograph",
      "grade": "derivative-Arabic-to-working-English-selected-locus-review",
      "historical_print_verified": false,
      "manuscript_verified": false
    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-026",
      "page": 138,
      "ar": "فالغالب على الروح أثر الحكم الأول ، وعلى النفس أثر الحكم الثاني",
      "en": "The first judgment predominates in spirit, and the second in the soul.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "Differentiates the two modes under which outward and inward mirrors receive disclosure.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-030",
      "page": 140,
      "ar": "ثم يعود الانتهاء إلى الابتداء لإتمام الدائرة",
      "en": "The ending returns to the beginning, completing the circle.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "At higher realization beginnings and endings are gathered, rather than collapsed into a time sequence.",

    },
    {
      "id": "CR011-031",
      "page": 141,
      "ar": "بحيث لا تظهر غلبة شيء من الأسماء على شيء أصلا",
      "en": "So that no single name prevails over another at all.",

      "critical_note": "Muhammadan perfection is modeled as an equilibrium and comprehensive inclusiveness.",

    {

    {

      "page": 142,
      "ar": "فالأول سمّي مناسبة ومحبة فعلية",
      "en": "The first of these is called relational affinity and love through action.",

      "page": 143,
      "ar": "علمنا بأن رؤيته أولا رؤية فعل لا رؤية عين",

    {

      "page": 144,
      "ar": "والرابعة : رتبة التحقيق وهي رتبة الخلافة من وجه والكمال من جميع الوجوه",
      "en": "Fourth is realization: vicegerency in one regard, perfection in every regard.",

    {
      "id": "CR011-040",
      "page": 145,
      "ar": "إحداها : من جهة الفعل الرابط بين الفاعل والمفعول",
      "en": "First, from the act linking agent and that upon which the agent acts.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "First of three aspects of the bond established by love.",
      "source_siglum": "D-Forum-B2007-derivative",

    {

      "page": 145,
      "ar": "وثانيها : من جهة الصفة التي هي القدر المشترك",
      "en": "Second, from the attribute that is held in common.",
      "disposition": "retain",
      "critical_note": "The common predicate is analogical/relational in the author's argument, not univocal identity of Creator and creature.",

    {

      "page": 145,
      "ar": "وثالثها : من جهة الذات التي هي عين الوجود",
      "en": "Third, from Essence, which is the very reality of existence.",
      "disposition": "annotate",
      "critical_note": "Metaphysical terminology requires contextual attribution: this is Farghānī's Akbarī doctrinal claim, not an unqualified collapse of creature and Creator.",

    {

      "page": 145,
      "ar": "ثم بالرجوع من البداية إلى النهاية ، وبالجمع بين أحكامهما تبيّنت رتبة الكمال والتحقيق",
      "en": "Through returning from beginning to end and bringing their judgments together, the rank of perfection and realization becomes apparent.",
~~~
